It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a disciplined report-style design: a contents page, six numbered full-bleed dividers (4, 9, 21, 32, 49, 55) and a consistent takeaway headline over one main chart on most pages, so the route is easy to follow and each page contributes. Typography, colour-coded highlights and chart treatment are consistent, and slides 10, 33 and 23 read cleanly at size. Weaknesses are real: slide 5 has a mislabelled dual x-axis (1992 to 2036 above, 2018 to 2036 below) and a 'log scale' that is hard to decode, and a few pages (26, 29, 47, 48, 52) carry unreadable or cluttered content, plus 3D pies on 31 and tiny footers throughout.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the numbered section dividers, the takeaway headline plus single large chart pattern, and the highlighted key phrases that give each page one clear message.

**Most useful repair:** Fix the slide 5 axis and annotation so the internet-versus-crypto comparison reads at a glance, and redraw the 3D pies on slide 31 as flat charts.

**What would change the score:** I would cut or simplify the dense pages (47 table, 48 link lists, 52 matrix, 29 logo wall) and enlarge the footnotes and small chart labels that are illegible at normal viewing size.

_Confidence high. 58 of 58 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide, a legal disclosures page and a table of contents listing six sections. It then covers them in order: how big crypto is today, including active addresses, mobile wallet growth and an estimate of real users; why crypto has become a political issue, including bipartisan quotes, swing-state search interest, regulation and central bank digital currencies; the maturity of infrastructure, including multichain flows, cheaper Ethereum layer-two fees, account abstraction and zero knowledge proofs; the key applications emerging, including stablecoins, AI, NFTs, DeFi, prediction markets, DePIN, gaming and social; what is known about builders; and finally a look at what comes next. Each section opens with a full-bleed divider slide carrying a numbered label. Most content slides use a headline-style title stating the takeaway, followed by one large chart or a few data callouts with source notes beneath. The deck closes with forward-looking thoughts, a price-cycle chart and a resources page.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2024 deck have?**

The deck has 58 pages. Its table of contents on slide 3 lists six sections: how big crypto is today, why crypto has become a political issue, infrastructure maturity, key applications, what is known about builders, and what comes next.

**What does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2024 deck say about monthly active crypto addresses?**

Slide 5 says monthly active addresses hit an all-time high of 220 million. It adds that one address does not necessarily correspond to one person.

**What does the a16z crypto deck estimate about the number of active crypto users?**

Slide 8 estimates that only 5 to 10% of crypto owners are active users, which it puts at 30 to 60 million estimated monthly active users. It counts 617 million estimated global crypto owners on the same slide.

**What does the a16z crypto State of Crypto 2024 deck say about the cost of sending USD internationally?**

Slide 24 lists $44 via international wire transfer, $12 via USDC on Ethereum at the 2021 average gas price, $1 via USDC on Ethereum at the September 2024 average gas price, and less than $0.01 via USDC on Base L2 at the September 2024 average gas price. The slide notes that these figures may exclude some onboarding and exit costs.

### Slide 4

**Text**

01 / State of Crypto 2024 /
How big is / crypto today?
©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.
4

**Layout**

Left-aligned composition with a 2.5% left margin: a small eyebrow line sits above a large two-line title in the vertical middle-left of the slide. The a16z crypto logo sits bottom-left above the copyright line, and the slide number sits bottom-right. The background is full-bleed with no title bar, grid or columns visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed generative background illustration: a soft blurred gradient moving from green at top-left, through teal and deep teal at top-right, to yellow and golden ochre at the bottom. Over the middle band (about 23% to 78% of slide height) sits a semi-transparent mosaic of rectangular blocks in rows and columns, each block a slightly different tint of green, yellow or teal, so the gradient shows through. No people, objects or photographs. Logo: the a16z crypto wordmark in white, lowercase, bottom-left.

**Design**

Background is a multi-colour gradient with a translucent pixel-block mosaic band in the middle. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Noto Sans ExtraBold (humanist sans-serif, heavy weight), about 14.3% of slide height per line, tight leading with two lines. Eyebrow text is navy #12335A in Noto Sans Medium, about 3.0% of slide height. Footer and page number are light grey #D9D9D9 and #EBEBEB in Noto Sans Medium, about 1.2% to 1.5% of slide height. No shapes, lines, corner radii or shadows. Gradient background from green #4FA37A to teal #12A899 to deep teal #0A5F77 at top and yellow #F2D033 to golden ochre #D9A90C at bottom.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A short headline callout on the left is paired with a single log-scale line chart on the right, so the takeaway reads before the data.
- Review: Weaknesses are real: slide 5 has a mislabelled dual x-axis (1992 to 2036 above, 2018 to 2036 below) and a 'log scale' that is hard to decode, and a few pages (26, 29, 47, 48, 52) carry unreadable or cluttered content, plus 3D pies on 31 and tiny footers throughout.
- Suggested repair: Fix the slide 5 axis and annotation so the internet-versus-crypto comparison reads at a glance, and redraw the 3D pies on slide 31 as flat charts.

**Text**

Monthly active / addresses hit an / all-time high of / 220 million — / with growth / reminiscent of / early internet / adoption / Note: One address does not / necessarily correspond to one person. / For more on how we think about / active addresses and users see here. / Internet users¹ vs. monthly active crypto addresses² (log scale) / 2005 / 1992 / 1993 / 1994 / 1995 / 1996 / 1997 / 1998 / 1999 / 2000 / 2001 / 2002 / 2003 / 2004 / 2006 / 2007 / 2008 / 2009 / 2010 / 1B / 100M / 10M / 1 billion internet users / 2031 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / 2026 / 2027 / 2028 / 2029 / 2030 / 2032 / 2033 / 2034 / 2035 / 2036 / Unique sending addresses as of Dec. of each year; 2024 as of Sept. Blockchains include / Aptos, Arbitrum, Avalanche C-Chain, Base, Bitcoin, Blast, BNB Chain, Celo, Ethereum, / Fantom, Linea, Near, OP Mainnet, Polygon PoS, Scroll, Solana, Sui, TON, Tron, zkSync, and / Zora. EVM addresses active on multiple chains only contribute once to the total. / Sources: 1/ World Bank 2/ Artemis and Dune (@DarenMatsuoka). / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide. / 5

**Layout**

Two columns on a 80/20 split: a narrow left panel about 0 to 30 percent of the width holds the headline (top, starting at about 8 percent from the top), a highlighted phrase, and a note block lower down (about 77 to 86 percent), with the alózcrypto wordmark and copyright line at the bottom-left; a vertical gradient rule separates it from the right panel, which holds a chart title across the top (about 4 percent from the top), a log-scale line chart from about 10 to 85 percent of the height, and a footnote and source line along the bottom. The title, note and chart title are left-aligned to the left edge of their columns; the slide number sits bottom-right at about 98 percent of the width and 96 percent of the height.

**Visuals**

One line chart rendered as an image (2000 by 1184 px) at x 34.2 y 14.2, size 62 by 61.7 percent. Type: multi-series line chart with a log-scale y axis and year on the x axis. Title (text above chart): 'Internet users¹ vs. monthly active crypto addresses² (log scale)'. Y axis labels (log scale, no units printed beyond the labels): 10M at bottom, 100M in the middle, 1B near the top; gridlines are light grey horizontal lines at these levels. X axis: calendar years 1992 to 2010 printed in green along the top of the chart and 2018 to 2036 printed in orange along the bottom; vertical light-grey gridlines at each year, with the 2031 column shaded by a grey band. Series 1 (green #6FB63E, labelled 'Internet users¹', markers as filled circles, line about 2.5 px): about 7M in 2018, about 10M in 2019, about 20M in 2020, about 38M in 2021, about 78M in 2022, about 118M in 2023, about 180M in 2024, about 280M in 2025, about 400M in 2026, about 500M in 2027, about 660M in 2028, about 790M in 2029, about 930M in 2030, about 1.0B in 2031, about 1.1B in 2032, about 1.3B in 2033, about 1.5B in 2034, about 1.7B in 2035, about 1.9B in 2036 (values after 2024 are estimated from the plotted positions). Series 2 (orange #FBB966, labelled 'monthly active crypto addresses²', filled circle markers): about 12M in 2018, about 14M in 2019, about 25M in 2020, about 52M in 2021, about 60M in 2022, about 73M in 2023, about 220M in 2024 (2024 as of Sept per footnote; values are estimates, no values printed). Legend: none as a separate box; series are identified by the coloured underlined words in the chart title. Highlighted points: the 2031 column is shaded grey and the annotation '1 billion internet users' (dark green #197152, bold, rotated about 90 degrees, reading upward) sits beside the 2031 point of the green series. The two series cross near 2024. Also present: a fill rectangle highlighting 'all-time high' in the headline and a wordmark logo (alózcrypto) at the bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Soft gradient from mint #BFEBDD at top-left through pale green to pale yellow #FFF9D6 at the bottom-right on the left panel; right panel plain white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 6

**Text**

Monthly active crypto addresses / Ethereum Virtual Machine (EVM) chains: 52M / Non-EVM chains: 174M / Addresses are / proliferating / across many / blockchains, / with Base and / Solana as the / most active / Base: 22M / BNB Chain: 10M / Ethereum: 6M / Polygon PoS: 5M / Arbitrum: 3M / OP Mainnet: 1M / Celo: 1M / Linea: 1M / Scroll: 900K / zkSync: 800K / Fantom: 400K / Avalanche C-Chain: 400K / Zora: 300K / Blast: 200K / Solana: 100M / NEAR: 31M / Tron: 14M / Bitcoin: 11M / TON: 10M / Sui: 5M / Aptos: 4M / 220+ million / Monthly active addresses / Note: One address does not / necessarily correspond to one person. / For more on how we think about / active addresses and users see here. / a16z crypto / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide. / Some of the above are a16z investments. For a full list see a16z.com/investment-list. / This content should not be considered investment advice. / EVM addresses active on multiple chains only contribute once to the total. / Sources: Artemis and Dune (@DarenMatsuoka) as of 9/30/2024. / 6

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: a narrow left panel (about 0 to 30% of width) holds the headline, a note and the a16z logo, separated by a thin vertical gradient rule at about 30% from a white right area. The right area has a chart title at top (about 32% x, 5.7% y) with a thin navy rule running right from about 61% to 99% x, then two side-by-side horizontal bar charts (EVM left from about 32% x, non-EVM right from about 66% x) with a large statistic bottom right; footers sit along the bottom in two columns (about 32% and 68% x). Left-aligned throughout, margins about 3% left and 1% right, page number 6 at bottom right (about 98% x, 96% y).

**Visuals**

Two horizontal bar charts with logos, no axes or gridlines. Left chart (EVM chains, total 52M, green bars, bar length proportional to value): Base 22M (longest, about 25% of slide width, label inside bar, right-aligned), BNB Chain 10M, Ethereum 6M, Polygon PoS 5M, Arbitrum 3M, OP Mainnet 1M, Celo 1M, Linea 1M, Scroll 900K, zkSync 800K, Fantom 400K, Avalanche C-Chain 400K, Zora 300K, Blast 200K; labels follow bar ends except Base and BNB Chain which sit inside bars. Right chart (Non-EVM chains, total 174M, orange bars): Solana 100M (label inside bar), NEAR 31M (inside), Tron 14M, Bitcoin 11M, TON 10M, Sui 5M, Aptos 4M, with bars shorter in proportion. Each bar has a round or square chain logo at its end: Base (blue circle), BNB Chain (gold cube), Ethereum (grey diamond), Polygon PoS (purple linked hexagons), Arbitrum (blue and white emblem), OP Mainnet (red circle with OP), Celo (yellow circle with C), Linea (black square with L), Scroll (scroll icon), zkSync (black circle with arrows), Fantom (blue hexagon), Avalanche C-Chain (red triangle circle), Zora (dark blue-grey sphere), Blast (black circle with yellow B), Solana (teal and purple stripes), NEAR (black N), Tron (red circle with triangle), Bitcoin (orange circle with B), TON (blue circle with diamond), Sui (light blue drop), Aptos (black and white striped circle). Statistic: 220+ million in heavy black sans text with Monthly active addresses below it. Left panel has a 1920 by 1080 gradient image and a 1152 by 648 full-slide background image. Logo placement: the a16z crypto wordmark is a light grey-white logotype at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Left panel mint-to-pale-yellow gradient (#CDEFE3 to #FDF9D0); right area solid white #FFFFFF. Left panel background is a soft gradient from mint green #CDEFE3 at top through pale green #E9F7EA to pale yellow #FDF9D0 at bottom; right area is pure white #FFFFFF. Thin vertical rule at about 30% x with gradient from teal #1F9E8A at top through yellow-green to gold #D9A700 at bottom. Headline and chart text are bold navy #12335A; the statistic and its caption are black #000000. Highlight behind 'many', 'blockchains', '52M' and '174M' is lime #E2FF6B. EVM bars green #6DB33F; non-EVM bars light orange #FDB462. Typeface is Noto Sans (bold for headlines and values, medium for chain names, regular for notes); headline text about 4.9% of slide height, chart labels about 1.9%, title about 3.0%, notes and footers about 1.4%. Thin navy rule about 0.1% high. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on the bars; logos have no frames.

### Slide 7

**Text**

Mobile wallet use hit record highs, driven by growth in countries / such as Nigeria, India, and Argentina / Monthly active mobile crypto wallet users by country / Total / As percent of total / France / 30M / 100% / Spain / Brazil / Canada / Turkey / Germany / UK / 20M / Indonesia / Pakistan / Philippines / Russia / 50% / Argentina / India / Nigeria / 10M / USA / Other / 0 / 0% / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 7 / Source: Sensor Tower as of 9/30/2024. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top (about 0 to 24 percent of slide height) holding a two-line left-aligned title starting at x 3.5 percent, with a thin yellow-to-green rule along its bottom edge. Below, a left-aligned subtitle sits at x 1.9 percent, y 24.8 percent, followed by two equal-height area charts side by side (left 'Total' chart x 1.3 to 42.4 percent, right 'As percent of total' chart x 46.4 to 87.5 percent) with a shared legend column at x 93 percent. Footer row at the bottom: logo at bottom left, copyright line beneath it, source line right-aligned near x 79.6 to 93.2 percent and slide number 7 at x 98.3 percent, all near y 96 percent.

**Visuals**

Two stacked area charts sharing one series order and one legend. Left chart: stacked area chart titled 'Total' (subtitle label, no separate chart title), y axis 0 to 30M with labelled ticks 0, 10M, 20M, 30M; x axis years 2018 to 2024 labelled at yearly ticks; values rise slowly from near zero in 2018 to about 8M in 2020, climb steeply through 2021 to about 14M, peak near 2022 at about 25M, dip to about 20M in 2023, then reach about 28M near late 2024 with a sharp spike. Right chart: 100 percent stacked area chart titled 'As percent of total', y axis 0% to 100% labelled 0%, 50%, 100%; x axis years 2018 to 2024; Other (grey) is about 45 percent in 2019 and about 35 percent in 2024; USA (slate blue) is about 40 percent of the total in 2018 to 2019, falling to about 25 percent around 2021 and about 20 percent by 2024; Nigeria, India and Argentina bands widen from 2020 onward, with India (pink) about 15 percent and Nigeria about 10 percent in 2023 to 2024; no numeric values are printed on the right chart, so all these are estimates. Legend (right of the charts, top to bottom): France orange #FF9A5A, Spain slate #5F6B7A, Brazil teal #6A9E8A, Canada yellow #FFE15A, Turkey aqua #8EDBE0, Germany light green #9BD17A, UK lavender #D9A3E8, Indonesia light pink #FFC4D3, Pakistan mauve #9E74B0, Philippines pale cyan #A8EEFA, Russia dark green #4E7F57, Argentina pale yellow-green #E5FA8A, India pink #F772B4, Nigeria peach #FCCF96, USA slate blue #5A8FA3, Other grey #B3B3B3. Highlighted: the phrase 'record highs' in the title sits on a lime highlight block. Logo: outlined grey wordmark 'alózcrypto' at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF body with a mint-to-pale-blue gradient header band across the top. Clean corporate-editorial look. Header band uses a pale mint-to-light-blue gradient (left #C8EEE0, centre near #EEF8EC, right #C5DDE5) with a navy title; the highlight behind 'record highs' is lime #E2FF6B. Title is bold humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), about 4.9 percent of slide height per line, navy #12335A, line spacing about 7.7 percent of slide height between title lines. Subtitle is Noto Sans Bold about 2.5 percent, navy #12335A. Chart captions and axis labels are Noto Sans Medium about 1.6 to 2.0 percent, navy #12335A for captions and grey #595959 for axis ticks; legend labels Noto Sans Medium 1.7 percent navy #12335A. Source line Noto Sans Regular 1.4 percent #595959; copyright line Noto Sans Medium 1.2 percent #D9D9D9. Chart areas have thin darker outlines (about 0.5 pt, slate grey #3A4A55) between bands. Header rule: horizontal gradient line from yellow #F5D83A to green #3E8A5E. No shadows, no rounded corners, no drop shadows on text.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 8

**Text**

We estimate that only 5–10% of crypto owners are active users — / there’s a big opportunity to re-engage passive holders onchain / 30–60M / Estimated monthly / active users⁴ / How do we estimate crypto users? / 1/ Apply filtering and de-duplication to active / addresses to remove bots and transient addresses. / 2/ Extrapolate using reported wallet users (e.g., / Metamask) and mobile wallet market share data. / For more on how we think about active addresses / and users see here. / 617M / Estimated global / crypto owners³ / 220M / Monthly active / addresses² / 27M / Monthly mobile / wallet users¹ / Sources: / 1/ Sensor Tower as of Sept 2024 / 2/ Artemis and Dune as of Sept 2024 / (EVM addresses active on multiple chains / only contribute once to the total) / 3/ Crypto.com as of June 2024 (refers to / people who own crypto, but are not / necessarily transacting onchain) / 4/ a16zcrypto.com/posts/article/ / estimating-crypto-users/ / 8 / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a header band across the top about 24% tall holding a two-line bold title left-aligned at x 3.5% and a thin gradient rule beneath it at about y 20%. Below, a large concentric semicircle graphic is centred on x 50% and rests on the bottom edge, with a left-hand text panel (x 5.8% to 35%) and a right-hand stack of figures (x 70% to 88%). A small logo sits bottom-left and the copyright line runs beside it at y 97%; the sources block sits bottom-right at x 81.6% to 96% and the page number 8 is at x 98.3%, y 96.2%.

**Visuals**

Concentric semicircular diagram (nested arches) centred at x 50%, base on the bottom edge. Outer arch: pale mint #D2EEE3 with a 21% #22AF78 tint over the lower area, spanning the full width from about y 24% down. Middle arch: muted teal #7CB9A8, spanning x 19.6% to 81.2%, top at y 56.4%. Inner arch: bright yellow-green #E2FF6B ring with a dotted dark outline, spanning x 36.1% to 63.9%, top at y 79%, containing a solid teal #00958B semicircle spanning x 39.2% to 60.8%, top at y 84.6%. Callout 1: a yellow #E2FF6B box with a dotted dark border at x 15.1% to 37.9%, y 35% to 53.1%, holding the figure 30–60M with the caption Estimated monthly active users⁴. Connector: a yellow #E2FF6B line about 3 px wide runs horizontally from the right edge of the callout (x 37.9%, y 40.9%) to x 50%, then straight down to the top of the inner yellow arch at y 79%. Figures in the diagram: 617M in teal #00958B at the outer arch right side, captioned Estimated global crypto owners³; 220M in white on the middle arch, captioned Monthly active addresses²; 27M in white on the inner teal arch, captioned Monthly mobile wallet users¹. No chart axes or legend. Translucent white panel #FFFFFF at 44% opacity behind the left text, x 3.6% to 37.9%, y 53.2% to 86.5%. Logo: the alozcrypto wordmark in light grey outline at bottom-left, x 2.2% to 9.8%, y 93.6% to 96.2%.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a mint-to-white-to-blue-grey gradient header band across the top (left #C8EDE0 to right #C6DDE3). Title and body text dark navy #12335A; accent yellow-green #E2FF6B for highlight and callouts; teal #00958B for the 617M figure and inner arch; mid teal #7CB9A8 for the middle arch. Typeface Noto Sans (humanist sans-serif): Bold for the title, figures and headings, Medium for list text, Regular for the closing line and sources. Title is about 4.75% of slide height per line; the big figure 30–60M about 8.9%; body text about 2.2%; sources about 1.4%. Callout box has a dotted dark outline; connector line is square-cornered. Header rule is a horizontal line about 0.5% tall with a yellow-to-green gradient (#F5D63A at left, #3F8F5E at right) at y about 20.2%. No shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 9

9

**Layout**

Full-bleed single-column composition with the text block left-aligned at about 2.5% from the left edge. A small black section label sits near the top of a horizontal band, the large white headline occupies the middle, and the a16z crypto logo and copyright footer sit bottom-left with the slide number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed abstract background image of soft pink-to-purple blurred gradient blobs at the top and bottom, with a central horizontal band of blocky mosaic-like rectangles in pink, magenta and violet. No charts, photographs of people or diagrams. One logo: the white a16z crypto wordmark at bottom-left, rendered as lowercase text 'a16zcrypto' with a stylised 'a' and 'z'. The band has no outline and the mosaic tiles are semi-transparent over the gradient.

**Design**

Background is a multicolour gradient of pink #F27FA8 at top-left fading to violet #8E2A9E at top-right and deep purple #4A1670 at bottom-right, with a central band of magenta mosaic tiles approximately #E0408F to #8A1A7A. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Noto Sans ExtraBold (sans-serif, heavy weight), about 14.3% of slide height per line, left-aligned. Section label is black #000000 in Noto Sans Medium, about 3% of slide height. Footer copyright is light grey #D9D9D9 in Noto Sans Medium, about 1.2% of slide height. Slide number is grey #999999 in Noto Sans Medium, about 1.5% of slide height. Accent colours are pink and violet; no shapes, lines, radii or shadows.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Quotes from many politicians are set in a grid beside a large bold pull phrase, turning a dense list of statements into a readable comparison.
- Review: Typography, colour-coded highlights and chart treatment are consistent, and slides 10, 33 and 23 read cleanly at size.

**Text**

“We will partner together to invest in America’s competitiveness, to invest in America’s future. We will encourage innovative technologies like AI and digital assets while protecting consumers and investors.” / > Vice President Kamala Harris
“This afternoon I’m laying out my plan to ensure that the United States will be the crypto capital of the planet and the bitcoin superpower of the world and we’ll get it done.” / > Republican presidential candidate Donald Trump
"We all believe in the future of crypto….I want to bring members on both sides of the aisle here in the Senate together...so we can pass sensible [crypto] legislation that helps the United States maintain its status as the most innovative country in the world." / > Sen. Chuck Schumer (D-NY)
“The U.S. has always been a financial leader, but we're on the brink of being left behind in the digital asset race….now Congress needs to pass essential legislation to secure our financial future.” / > Sen. Cynthia Lummis (R-WY)
“We’ve been working hard to develop a comprehensive cryptocurrency framework that enables entrepreneurs and businesses to innovate responsibly while also protecting consumers and cracking down on bad actors.” / > Sen. Kirsten Gillibrand (D-NY)
"Regardless of the outcome, I expect you to see digital asset legislation start to move in both bodies....I think it is ‘when,’ not ‘if,’ regardless of who’s in charge.” / > Majority Whip Tom Emmer (R-MN)
“These [crypto builders] are focused on innovating and creating jobs in the U.S. An already difficult task made tougher without clear guardrails or regulation.” / > Rep. Ro Khanna (D-CA)
“We'll continue fighting for rules and regulations for digital assets that promote innovation, protect consumers, and keep opportunity here in America, not overseas.” / > Sen. Tim Scott (R-S.C.)
Democrats / and / Republicans / recognize the / opportunity / for crypto / and are ready / to pass / bipartisan / legislation
Sources: whitehouse.gov, CNBC, cryptonews.com, X, CNBC, X, Decrypt, X

10

**Layout**

Two-column grid of eight quote cards on the left and right of a white field, with a narrow vertical gradient divider at about 75% of the width separating them from a full-height pink title panel on the right. Each card pairs a circular headshot (left column at about x 2%, right column at about x 40%) with a quote block and a magenta '>' attribution line. The right-panel title is left-aligned at about x 78%, top y 7%, and runs down to about y 73%. The sources line sits bottom-right of the quote area at y 96%, the copyright and logo sit bottom-left, and the page number sits bottom-right at x 98%, y 96%. Margins are about 2% left and right.

**Visuals**

Eight circular headshots of US politicians, each cropped to a circle with a thin dark frame, arranged in two columns of four: Kamala Harris (top left, smiling woman with dark hair and pearl necklace, navy background); Donald Trump (top right, man in dark suit, white shirt, blue tie, American flag behind); Chuck Schumer (second row left, man in grey suit and yellow tie, blurred background); Cynthia Lummis (second row right, smiling woman with blonde hair and patterned scarf, flag behind); Kirsten Gillibrand (third row left, smiling woman with blonde hair in black top, dark background); Tom Emmer (third row right, man in dark suit and red tie, flag pin, flag behind); Ro Khanna (bottom left, smiling man in dark suit, flag behind); Tim Scott (bottom right, smiling man in grey suit and red tie, teal background). Photos are head-and-shoulders portraits, soft front lighting, dominant colours navy, grey and white. A small grey outlined al6zcrypto wordmark sits bottom left. A magenta #E032E0 rectangle sits behind the word 'bipartisan' in the title panel, which is set in white text. Hyperlinked source names are underlined.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the left three-quarters, with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient (#FDE9F3 to #EEE4F8) panel on the right quarter. White background with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient panel on the right third. Quote text is regular sans-serif (Noto Sans Regular) in black #000000, about 2% of slide height. Magenta #E032E0 is used for highlighted phrases and '>' markers. Attribution names are Noto Sans Bold in grey #595959. The title is Noto Sans Bold in navy #12335A, about 4.9% of slide height, with the word 'bipartisan' in white on a #E032E0 block. Shapes are flat with no shadows or rounded corners; the divider is a thin vertical gradient bar from purple to pink.

**Layout**

Single-column title block across the top with a two-line bold headline left-aligned at a 3.5% left margin, followed by a thin gradient rule at about 24% height; below it a left half holds a choropleth map of the United States and a right column holds a two-part ranked bar chart. A source footnote sits bottom right, the Andreessen Horowitz copyright sits bottom left, and the slide number sits at bottom right edge.

**Visuals**

Chart 1: choropleth map of the contiguous United States with Alaska and Hawaii inset at lower left, states filled on a diverging scale from magenta (9.7) through white to dark green (14.3). Highlighted states are extruded in 3D with bright yellow-green #E2FF6B side faces and drop shadows: Nevada (-2.0), Arizona (-3.0), Wisconsin (+4.7), Michigan (+3.3), Pennsylvania (+5.3, extruded at the right-centre Mid-Atlantic position), and Georgia (+0). Legend: horizontal gradient bar with left label 9.7 and right label 14.3. Chart 2: horizontal bar chart titled Change in rank from 2020–2024¹, shown in two stacked panels. Top panel on a light teal #00958B at 20% opacity background: North Dakota, Arkansas, Minnesota, Pennsylvania, Wisconsin, with green bars (+14.3 printed at Arkansas row, the longest bar is North Dakota) and bars continuing down through New Hampshire to California in medium green #7DB36A, shading toward pale green. Bottom panel with a vertical dark grey axis line: Alabama through New Mexico in pink-magenta #E8539A bars, with the longest negative bar New Mexico labelled -9.7. Values for unlabelled bars are not printed; the bars decline in length in list order. Highlighted labels: Pennsylvania, Wisconsin, Michigan, Georgia, Nevada, Arizona with yellow-green #E2FF6B highlight boxes. Logo: al6zcrypto wordmark, light grey-white, bottom left.

**Design**

Background #FBFBFB with a pink-to-lavender gradient band across the top about 24% of slide height (left #FCE9F0 blush pink, right #F3E9F8 lavender). Gradient rule below band runs from pink #FF6B9D to purple #8A2BB0. Title is bold NotoSans Bold in dark navy #142C47, about 4.9% of slide height per line, with the phrase swing states highlighted in #E2FF6B. Body and labels are NotoSans Bold in navy #12335A; subtitle sizes about 2.5% and 2.0%. Footnote is NotoSans Regular in grey #595959 at 1.4%. Shapes have square corners; map extrusions have soft shadows; bars are flat with no outline.

### Slide 12

**Text**

40+ million
Americans own
crypto¹ — they
are young,
bipartisan, and
supportive of
pro-crypto
candidates
25% of Americans aged 18–34 own crypto
51% say they’re likely to throw their weight
behind crypto-friendly candidates
In swing states, 41% identify as Democrats, 39%
identify as Republicans, and 20% identify as
Independent or Other
1/ Source: Morning Consult as of April 2024. Reports from Pew Research in 2021, 2022, and 2023 provide a similar estimate, a figure the White House also used in a
2022 fact sheet. However, a 2024 Federal Reserve study based on data from 2023 reports that the number may be slightly lower at 18 million.
All other stats were sourced from the Coinbase State of Crypto Report as of August 2024.
12
©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a narrow left column (about 0 to 30% of width) holds the stacked headline, and a wide right column (about 30 to 100%) holds three rows, each a pie chart at left followed by a statistic sentence, with the footnotes in the lower right. The headline sits top-left with its left edge at about 3.3% and its top at about 6%, and text is left-aligned throughout. A thin vertical gradient strip at about 29.8 to 30.3% separates the columns. The footer logo and copyright line sit bottom-left at about 2% margin, the source notes sit bottom-right starting at about 33.5%, and the page number sits at the bottom-right corner at about 97.8%, 96.2%.

**Visuals**

Three pie charts, stacked vertically in the right column, none with a title, axis, or legend. Pie 1 (top, about x 33.8 y 4.5 w 8.5 h 13.2 percent): a circle split into a quarter slice in light teal #4FB0BE at the top-right and three quarters in dark teal #295E66; the slice is labelled by the adjacent text 25%. Pie 2 (middle, about x 33.8 y 37 w 8.5 h 13.2): a circle split into a right half in violet #9050D8 and a left portion in dark purple #4B2A70, about 51% and 49%; no printed values beyond 51%. Pie 3 (bottom, about x 33.8 y 74 w 8.5 h 15): a circle with a blue #0044FF wedge of 41% on the upper right, a red #FF0000 wedge of 39% on the lower left, and a grey #999999 wedge of 20% at the upper left; the three wedges correspond to the coloured numbers in the adjacent sentence. Pie wedges have no outlines. Left panel: a pink-to-lavender vertical gradient field with no photograph or pattern, plus a thin magenta-to-violet vertical gradient strip at the column boundary. Logo: the al6zcrypto wordmark in pale grey outline, about 2.2% from left, at y 93.6, w 7.6, h 2.6 (small, low-contrast).

**Design**

Background is white on the right and a soft pink gradient (about #FCE8F3 at top to #EBD3EA at bottom) on the left column, with a vertical magenta-to-violet strip (#E0479E to #7B2FBE) at the boundary. Headline and body text are bold geometric humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), dark navy #12335A. Highlight behind the words 40+ million and Americans is a solid magenta #E032E0 rectangle with white text. Accent colours: 25% teal #4FB0BE, 51% violet #9050D8, 41% blue #0044FF, 39% red #FF0000, 20% grey #999999. Title size is about 4.9% of slide height with a line pitch of about 6.8%; statistic text is about 4% of slide height with line pitch about 6%. Footnotes are Noto Sans regular, about 1.2% of slide height, grey #555555. Copyright line is Noto Sans Medium about 1.2% in very light grey #D9D9D9. Page number is Noto Sans Medium about 1.5% in grey #999999. No shapes have corner radii, shadows or gradients other than the left panel and strip.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Major crypto policy calls for input
2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 YTD
48 / 30 / 20 / 5 / 4 / 4
By topic / By authority / By jurisdiction
Market structure & rules (60) / Stablecoins (26) / Illicit finance (12) / DeFi (6) / Tax (5) / Energy & climate (2)
EU Banking Authority (17) / EU Securities & Markets Authority (8) / UK Financial Conduct Authority (6) / UK HM Treasury (5) / Australian Treasury (4) / Other (71)
EU (26) / UK (19) / USA (16) / EU Ntl. Authority (16) / UAE (5) / Other (29)
The EU and UK lead when it comes to public engagement on crypto policy and regulation
Source: a16z crypto analysis as of 9/30/2024.
©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.
13

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: a wide white content area on the left (about 75% of the width) and a narrow pink-tinted panel on the right (about 25%) separated by a thin vertical purple-to-pink gradient rule at about x 75%. The chart title sits top-left at about y 7%, with a thin navy rule running to the right margin. Below the full-width bar chart, three columns of horizontal bar panels (By topic, By authority, By jurisdiction) sit left-aligned from a left margin of about 2%, each with a heading and thin navy rule. The right panel holds the large headline, top-aligned at about y 7%, left-aligned at about x 78%. The a16z logo is bottom-left, the copyright line beneath it, the source line bottom-right of the content area, and the page number bottom-right of the slide.

**Visuals**

Chart 1: vertical bar chart titled 'Major crypto policy calls for input', six pink bars for 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023 and 2024 YTD, each with a value label above it: 2019 = 5, 2020 = 4, 2021 = 4, 2022 = 20, 2023 = 48, 2024 YTD = 30. No axis numbers are printed; the category axis is at the bottom with no unit given. Five light grey horizontal gridlines run across the plot at about 20% intervals of the plot height, with the baseline darker. Bars are pink #F9AAC1 with a slightly darker pink #E88FA8 outline. No legend and no highlighted points. Bar panel 'By topic': cyan horizontal bars in #8DEEFA, labelled Market structure & rules (60) (longest), Stablecoins (26), Illicit finance (12), DeFi (6), Tax (5), Energy & climate (2); labels for the first two sit inside the bar end, the rest sit just right of the bar. Bar panel 'By authority': teal #5FCFD6 bars, labelled EU Banking Authority (17), EU Securities & Markets Authority (8), UK Financial Conduct Authority (6), UK HM Treasury (5), Australian Treasury (4), Other (71) (longest, label inside bar end). Bar panel 'By jurisdiction': dark purple #653A7E bars, labelled EU (26), UK (19), USA (16), EU Ntl. Authority (16), UAE (5), Other (29) (longest). Bar lengths are proportional to the values within each panel. Logo: a16z crypto wordmark in light grey outline, bottom-left. Right panel: a soft pink-to-lilac gradient background image with faint decorative shapes at the bottom right.

**Design**

Background: white on the left content area; right panel pale pink gradient from #FDE8F2 at top to #F1DCEF at bottom. Headline and title text: navy #12335A, NotoSans Bold. Body and labels: navy #12335A, NotoSans Bold for bar labels and chart title. Section headings (By topic, By authority, By jurisdiction) in NotoSans Medium navy #12335A. Axis labels and source in grey #595959. Accent colours: magenta highlight #E032E0 behind 'EU and UK' in the headline (white text on magenta), pink #F9AAC1 bars, cyan #8DEEFA, teal #5FCFD6 and purple #653A7E bar panels. Title size about 2.96% of slide height for the chart title and about 4.94% for the headline; body and bar labels about 1.85%; footer about 1.2 to 1.4%. Lines: thin navy 1 px rules beside headings; vertical divider is a gradient bar from purple #7B2FA0 to pink #F06FA0. No corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 14

**Text**

The crypto industry is on a path toward more regulatory clarity / after some major policy wins in 2024 / Wyoming Decentralized / Unincorporated Nonprofit / Association Act (DUNA) / March 2024 / Signed into law with an effective date / of July 1, 2024 / Recognizes DAOs as legal entities, / enabling blockchain networks to operate / lawfully without compromising their / decentralization / U.S. Financial Innovation / and Technology for the 21st / Century Act (FIT21) / May 2024 / Passed the House with bipartisan / support, including 71 Democrat votes / Provides regulatory clarity for crypto, / protects consumers from bad actors, / and promotes American innovation — / pending Senate approval / EU Markets in / Crypto-Assets Regulation / (MiCA) / December 2024 / Goes fully into effect, after approval / by a large majority in 2023 / The European Union’s first / comprehensive regulatory framework / involving crypto assets and related / service providers / 14 / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a bold two-line headline across the top (left-aligned, starting at about 3.5% from the left edge) above a thin gradient rule at about 21% of the height. Below, three equal columns of about 30% width separated by two thin vertical navy rules, each column holding a small icon, a highlighted white-on-magenta title, a bold date line, a regular description and an italic description, all left-aligned. The alchemy-style footer logo sits bottom-left and the copyright line is beneath it; the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three square icon images, one per column, each about 3.7% wide and 6.5% high. Left column: dark purple (#3B1F5C) block pattern forming an arch-like motif. Middle column: mint green (#6EDBA0) pattern of circles and curved shapes with thin grey outlines. Right column: cyan (#3ED8E6) square tile pattern with octagonal and rounded shapes and white gaps. A small al6zcrypto wordmark logo in light grey outline sits bottom-left. No charts, photographs or tables; the header is a soft pink-to-lavender gradient band and the rule under it runs pink to purple.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band across the top (about 0 to 21% of height). White content area with a pale pink (#FDE9F3) to lavender (#F6E6F7) gradient header band across the top; a gradient rule (pink #F0609A to purple #8E2DA0) sits under it. Headline and body text are dark navy #12335A. Title highlights are magenta #E032E0 blocks behind white bold text. Typeface is Noto Sans (humanist sans-serif): Bold for the headline, title highlights and date lines, Regular for body, Italic for descriptions, Medium for page number and copyright. Headline cap height is about 4.9% of slide height, column titles about 3.5%, body text about 2.7%, footer about 1.2%. Vertical dividers are thin navy lines, no shadows or corner radii.

**Layout**

Two-panel split slide. A narrow left panel (about 0 to 30% of width) holds the title top-left at x 3.3%, the note bottom-left at x 3%, and the a16z crypto logo and copyright at the bottom-left margin. The right area (about 30 to 100% of width) is split by a thin horizontal rule at about y 52.7% into two stacked chart panels: Bitcoin on top and Ethereum below, each with a headline total at its right. The chart title sits top-left of the right area, footnote bottom-left of that area, source line bottom-right, and page number 15 at bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Two stacked vertical bar charts of daily net flows. Top chart: Bitcoin and Ethereum exchange-traded products (ETPs) daily net flows, bars in green (#7AB648-like), y axis in US dollars from -$2B to $2B with gridlines at $2B, $1B, $0, -$1B, -$2B; x axis labelled by months Feb, Mar, Apr, May, Jun, Jul, Aug, Sep (no unit labels). Series label box 'Bitcoin ETPs listed January 10th, 2024' with green fill. Highest bar about +$1.6B in early August; lowest bar about -$1.5B in late July/early August; a prominent spike about +$1.1B in March and about +$0.9B in June. Legend: none beyond the series label. Highlighted: the Aug spike and its negative counterpart. Bottom chart: Ethereum ETPs daily net flows, bars in orchid purple (#C86FD6-like), y axis from -$200M to $200M with gridlines at $200M, $100M, $0, -$100M, -$200M; no x axis labels; series label 'Ethereum ETPs listed July 24th, 2024' with magenta-purple fill. Bars begin around late July with largest about +$110M and lowest about -$155M; a late positive about +$70M near end of series. Total callouts: $58B in green for Bitcoin and $7B in purple for Ethereum, each followed by 'Total onchain holdings'.

**Design**

Background: Pink-to-lavender vertical gradient on the left panel (#FCEAF3 to #EBD3E6) with plain white (#FFFFFF) on the right chart area. Left panel background is a soft gradient from light pink (#FCEAF3) at top to lavender-pink (#EBD3E6) at bottom, with a thin vertical magenta-to-purple accent stripe (#E0408F to #8A2BB0) on its right edge around x 30%. Right area is plain white. Title text is bold humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), navy #12335A, about 4.9% of slide height per line, with the words Bitcoin and Ethereum set white on magenta #E032E0 highlight boxes. Body note is Noto Sans Regular, navy #12335A, about 2.1% of height. Chart title is bold navy #12335A. Chart axis labels grey #666666. Bar colours green and orchid. Thin grey horizontal rule separating charts in slate blue #3D5A80. Copyright line in light grey #D9D9D9, page number in grey #999999.

### Slide 16

**Text**

The U.S. dollar faces competition from foreign sovereign digital / currencies / Status of worldwide central bank digital currencies (CBDCs)¹ / CBDC developments / between banks have / doubled since Russia’s / invasion of Ukraine and / resulting sanctions.² / There are now 13 cross-border / projects between banks, / including mBridge, which / connects Thailand, China, Hong / Kong, UAE, Saudi Arabia, and / others. / Cancelled / Research / Proof of concept / Pilot / Launched / 16 / Sources: 1/ cbdctracker.org as of 9/30/2024 2/ Atlantic Council CBDC Tracker as of 9/30/2024. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-width title block across the top on a pale pink-to-white gradient band with a thin pink-to-purple rule beneath it at about 25% of slide height. A chart subtitle sits at left above a world choropleth map that spans the left ~70% of the slide, with a bold statement and body paragraph in a right-hand column starting at x 73%. Legend sits bottom-left over the map; logo bottom-left, copyright beneath it, sources line bottom-right with slide number at far right.

**Visuals**

Choropleth world map of CBDC status by country, equirectangular projection with Antarctica absent. Countries coloured by status: Cancelled in coral red (#E8828A-ish) for Ecuador-area and Kenya-area countries; Research in pale mint green (#A8DCCB-ish) for Mexico, much of Africa, Central Asia, Argentina, Chile, Pakistan-area, Korea-area; Proof of concept in lavender purple (#A896D6-ish) for Canada, USA, Alaska, Norway, Ukraine, Brazil, Australia, Thailand, Indonesia, Japan-area and New Zealand; Pilot in peach tan (#EBC9A4-ish) for Russia, Western Europe, Saudi Arabia, India, Mongolia and China; Launched in bright blue (#5FA8F0-ish) for Nigeria and Bahamas-area Zambia-area country near southern Africa. Greenland and unclassified countries shown in very pale grey-blue (#F3F6FB-ish). Legend: five square swatches with labels Cancelled, Research, Proof of concept, Pilot, Launched. Map has no highlighted points beyond the highlighted word; no axes, no photograph, no logos inside the map.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF body with pale pink-to-white gradient header band at top. White body background with a pale pink (#FCEBF2) to lavender-white gradient header band at top. Title in bold humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), colour navy #12335A, about 4.9% of slide height per line. Body text Noto Sans Medium/Regular, navy #12335A, about 2.6% of slide height. Accent highlight: 'doubled' set in white text on magenta fill #E035E0-ish. Thin gradient rule from pink #FF6B9D to purple #8E2DA8 at about 25.4% height. Source line in grey #595959, copyright in light grey #D9D9D9, slide number grey #999999. Logo wordmark 'al6zcrypto' in outlined light grey. No shadows, no corner radii.

**Layout**

Single-column title band across the top with the headline set flush left at about 3.5% margin, spanning two lines, above a thin gradient rule at about 21% of height. Below, a left block of charts (one pie with an area chart to its right on top, three pies in a row beneath) sits beside a dotted-border panel on the right holding a single large pie; the legend box sits top-right of the left block. Footer: logo bottom-left, copyright beneath it, sources bottom-right and page number 17 at far right.

**Visuals**

Chart 1 (top-left): pie chart titled Currency share in the global economy¹ ² with a caption Foreign exchange reserves beneath it. Pie about 58% US dollar (purple, label 58% USD), with slices for Euro (light cyan, about 15%), Japanese yen (light yellow-green, about 5%), Chinese renminbi (red-orange, about 2%) and Other (light grey, about 10%). A translucent purple-to-lavender wedge connects the pie to Chart 1b. Chart 1b: filled area chart of the US dollar share, y-axis 55% to 65% with gridlabels at 55%, 60%, 65%, x-axis years 2016 to 2024. Values about: 2016 about 65.5%, 2017 about 65%, 2018 about 63%, 2019 about 62%, 2020 about 61%, 2021 about 59%, 2022 about 59%, 2023 about 59%, 2024 about 58%. Area fill dark purple #633A7E, no outline. Legend box (navy #12335A outline, thin 1px border, white fill) with entries US dollar (purple square), Japanese yen (yellow-green square), Other (light grey square), Euro (cyan square), Chinese renminbi (red-orange square). Row of three pies beneath: International debt pie 64% USD (purple), Euro about 15%, Yen about 3%, Renminbi about 2%, Other about 16%; International loans pie 52% USD, Other about 22%, Yen about 3%, Euro about 17%; International payments (SWIFT) pie 48% USD, Euro about 25%, Yen about 5%, Renminbi about 3%, Other about 12%. Each pie has its USD label in white bold inside the purple slice, and a caption below in navy. Right panel: large pie titled Currency share of stablecoins³ with label >99% USD in white centred on a purple slice filling nearly the whole pie; a very thin cyan sliver at the top for Euro. Legend beneath the pie: cyan square 0.20% Euro, yellow-green square 0.007% Yen, red square 0.002% Renminbi, light grey square 0.04% Other. Logo: a16z crypto wordmark in pale grey outline at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF background with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band across the top. Light and clean. Right panel has a dotted grey border (#8A9BA8) with square corners. Chart palette: purple #633A7E, cyan #8AEBF9, yellow-green #DFF75A, red-orange #FF3B0A, light grey #E8E8E8. A thin grey vertical line sits at the right edge.

### Slide 18

**Text**

**Layout**

Full-width title band across the top with a two-line headline left-aligned at about 3.5% from the left edge, separated from the body by a thin gradient rule at about 25% height. Below, a horizontal bar chart occupies the left ~65% with its subtitle above it, and a right column (about x 70% to 97%) stacks two stablecoin cards (Tether, then USDC), each with a logo, name, large green figure, and italic launch year; the slide number sits bottom right and the Sources line sits bottom right beside it, with the a16z logo and copyright bottom left.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart titled 'Stablecoins compared to major foreign holders of U.S. Treasury securities'. Categories top to bottom with bars in purple (#6B3A7D approx.) except Stablecoins in green (#6FB540 approx.): Japan about $1.7T, China about $1.1T, United Kingdom about $0.98T, Luxembourg about $0.38T, Canada about $0.37T, Cayman Islands about $0.31T, Belgium about $0.31T, Ireland about $0.30T, France about $0.30T, Switzerland about $0.27T, Taiwan about $0.26T, India about $0.25T, Brazil about $0.24T, Hong Kong about $0.24T, Singapore about $0.24T, Norway about $0.19T, Saudi Arabia about $0.18T, South Korea about $0.17T, Mexico about $0.15T, Stablecoins about $0.14T (about $81B plus $11B, green), Germany about $0.14T. Value axis along the bottom labelled $250B, $500B, $1T, $1.5T, $2T with light grey vertical gridlines; no axis unit title; values are not printed on the bars, so bar lengths are estimates. No legend. A pale green-to-white gradient wedge fans from the Stablecoins bar toward the right edge of the chart. Tether logo: teal-green circle (#52AD8F approx.) with white Tether T mark. USDC logo: blue circle (#2775CA approx.) with white dollar-sign coin mark. Thin dark navy vertical rule at x about 69.5% separates chart from cards.

Background: White (#FFFFFF) with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band across the top. **Layout**

Two-column split slide. A narrow pink gradient panel fills the left ~30% of the slide full height, holding a left-aligned title block starting near the top-left margin (about 3% from the left edge, 7% from the top) and the logo and copyright footer at the bottom-left. The right ~70% is a white area holding a single combo chart with its bold title across the top, two callout boxes inside the plot, a two-line footnote at lower left and a source line at lower right. Slide number '19' sits bottom-right at about 98% x, 96% y.

**Visuals**

One combination chart with two series sharing a time x-axis. Title: 'Argentine peso (ARS) purchasing power in USD vs. stablecoin trading value with ARS on Bitso'. Left y-axis: 'purchasing power' in USD per ARS, linear, $0.000 to $0.008 with gridlines at $0.002 steps. Right y-axis: stablecoin trading value in USD millions, $0 to $80M with ticks at $20M steps. X-axis: time, labelled Dec 2022, Jun 2023, Dec 2023, Jun 2024, about 20 monthly points. Series 1 (line, magenta-purple #C86BD6, about 2.5 px stroke): purchasing power, starting about $0.0064 in Dec 2022, falling steadily to about $0.0030 by Dec 2023 and about $0.0011 by Jun 2024; no printed values. Series 2 (teal #5FD0D8 bars, about 20 bars): trading value, about $0.2M, $0.6M, $0.0M, $0.2M, $0.9M, $0.9M, $0.9M, $0.9M, $1.3M, $2.4M, $4.5M, $8.7M, $7.6M, $9.2M, $11.7M, $28.7M, $63.6M, $62.1M, $61.2M, $50.7M (estimates; the slide prints no bar values). Highlights: callout '>10,000% increase' with its phrase highlighted in lime #DDF55A; callout '82% decrease' with its phrase on red #E8452C fill and white text. No legend. Callouts are white rounded rectangles with thin grey #B8B8B8 outlines, placed over the plot area.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF on the right, with a pink gradient panel (#FDEFF7 to #EBD3EA) on the left 30 percent of the slide.

**Layout**

Two equal columns below a full-width header band; the title is left-aligned at about 3.5% from the left edge, set on two lines in the top 21% of the slide. A thin vertical navy divider at about 48.8% separates the left chart column (FDIC bank count with donut inset) from the right chart column (Nasdaq 100 market cap area chart with a logo column at the right edge). Each column has an underlined bold chart heading at about 24% from top. The source note sits bottom-right of the right column, the a16z wordmark and copyright sit bottom-left, and the page number '20' sits bottom-right at the margin.

**Visuals**

Left chart: vertical bar chart titled 'Number of FDIC-insured banks¹', solid black bars (#000000) on a white plot with light grey horizontal gridlines at 5K intervals. Y axis: number of banks, 0 to 20K, labels 0, 5K, 10K, 15K, 20K. X axis: years, labelled every four years 1992, 1996, 2000, 2004, 2008, 2012, 2016, 2020, 2024; bars run from about 1990 to 2024 (about 35 bars). No bar has printed values; estimates: first bar (1990) about 15,100, 1992 about 13,900, 1996 about 11,400, 2000 about 9,800, 2004 about 8,900, 2008 about 8,000, 2012 about 6,800, 2016 about 5,700, 2020 about 4,800, 2024 about 4,500 banks. No bars are highlighted. Overlaid callout box reads '5' (teal numeral) with 'banks control nearly half of all assets in the U.S. as the number of banks shrinks'. Inset donut chart titled 'By assets²' (source footnote 2 Federal Reserve as of 6/30/2024): ring with a light grey segment labelled 'All Other Banks' covering the left and upper-left of the ring, and an orange (#F2B76B) segment covering the right side with logos inside it top to bottom: CHASE (octagon mark), Bank of America (flag mark with BANK OF AMERICA wordmark), WELLS FARGO (black square badge), citibank (wordmark), usbank (wordmark with red-and-white mark). The donut hole is white. A grey gradient wedge runs from the bottom of the orange segment down-right to the top of the 2024 bar. Right chart: stacked area chart titled 'Market cap of the Nasdaq 100³' (source footnote 3 CapIQ and YCharts as of 9/30/2024), 2008 to about 2025 on the x axis with labels 2008, 2010, 2012, 2014, 2016, 2018, 2020, 2022, 2024; y axis 0% to 100% with labels 0%, 50%, 100%. Series from bottom to top, all estimates: dark teal #2E6F8E (Apple) about 5% in 2008 rising to about 16% in 2024; green #7DBA52 (Microsoft) above it, about 8% thick in 2008 and about 10% by 2024; purple #6F4B8F (Amazon) about 3% to 7%; light blue #9BEBF8 (Facebook) thin band; pink #F8B0C6 (Alphabet) about 5% to 15%; pale yellow-green #E2F57A (NVIDIA) top band, about 1% in 2008 and about 8% in 2024; light grey #EBEBEB region above all series is 'All other companies', about 80% in 2008 and about 37% in 2024. Callout box reads '6' (teal numeral) with 'Tech companies make up more than half the Nasdaq 100’s value'. Right-edge logo column stacked top to bottom: NVIDIA wordmark, Google G, Facebook circle f, Amazon smile logo, Microsoft four-square logo with word Microsoft, Apple logo, all in black. Bottom-left: a16z crypto wordmark in outline grey type.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band across the top 21% of the slide. Clean corporate-deck look. Header band has a pale pink to lavender gradient (#FDEEF4 at left to #F5E8F8 at right) with a 0.5% high rule below it running from pink #FF6FA8 at left to purple #8E2DBF at right. Title is bold humanist sans (Noto Sans Bold) in navy #12335A, about 4.9% of slide height for cap-height line; the word 'consolidate' sits on a magenta #E032E0 rectangle with white text. Body and callout text in Noto Sans Medium, navy #13335A, about 1.85% of slide height; axis labels Noto Sans Medium in grey #595959 at about 1.6%; footnotes Noto Sans Regular #595959 at about 1.36%; copyright Noto Sans Medium #D9D9D9 at about 1.2%; page number grey #999999. Chart headings Noto Sans Bold navy #12335A, about 3% of slide height, underlined. Accent numerals teal #24C5C9. Divider line thin navy #1F4E79 about 1 pixel. Callout boxes are white-grey with a thin grey #BFBFBF outline and small rounded corners, slight transparency. No shadows.

### Slide 21

03 / State of Crypto 2024 /

is maturing

©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-bleed section divider with left-aligned text in the left half. The kicker line '03 / State of Crypto 2024 /' sits above a two-line headline that starts at about 2.5% from the left edge. The a16z crypto wordmark sits bottom-left with the copyright line beneath it, and the page number sits bottom-right at about 96.5% x. No title bar or grid is used beyond these margins.

**Visuals**

Full-slide background illustration: a blurred colour-gradient field overlaid with a grid of semi-transparent square tiles. The top band is teal and green, fading to deep teal at top right, with a pale mint glow at upper left. The middle band is a tiled mosaic of squares, yellow at left shifting through lime to green and deep teal at right, in horizontal rows of about 85 px. The bottom is a darker mustard-to-ochre gradient. The image is full-bleed and covers the whole slide. Its duplicate placement is recorded once here. No charts, photographs or diagrams. One white a16z crypto logo in the bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed gradient of teal #1FA58F at top, yellow #E8C41A through the middle and ochre #C9A515 at bottom, overlaid with a translucent mosaic of colour tiles. Full-bleed gradient and mosaic background with no border. Headline is white #FFFFFF in Noto Sans ExtraBold (humanist sans-serif, bold weight), about 14.3% of slide height per line with tight leading (line spacing about 17% of slide height between baselines). Kicker is black #000000 in Noto Sans Medium, about 3.0% of slide height. Footer and page number are light grey #D9D9D9 or #EBEBEB in Noto Sans Medium, about 1.2 to 1.5% of slide height. Accent colours come from the background: teal about #12A896, yellow about #F2CB1B, and green about #7DB25A. No shapes, lines, shadows or corner radii.

### Slide 22

**Text**

Crypto is multichain — with value flowing between increasingly / interoperable blockchains / USD flows between various blockchains over last 12 months / $34B outflows / $28B inflows / $13B / Ethereum / Ethereum / $11B / $4.9B / $5.7B / $3.9B / $2.9B / $2.4B / $3.6B / $3.6B / $1.4B / $1.9B / $2.3B / $3.6B / $1.1B / 22 / Some of the above are a16z investments. For a full list see a16z.com/investment-list. This content should not be considered investment advice. / Source: Artemis as of 9/30/2024. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-width title block at the top with the headline set in two lines, left-aligned at about 3.5% from the left edge. A thin gradient rule sits at about 21% of the slide height. The chart sits in a wide band below the subtitle, with left and right node columns at about 3% and 87% of the width. The a16z wordmark sits bottom-left, the source line bottom-right and the page number at the far bottom-right corner, all on a shared footer baseline near 96% of the height.

**Visuals**

Sankey flow diagram titled 'USD flows between various blockchains over last 12 months', with values in USD billions. Left column of nodes, top to bottom: Ethereum (grey-blue box with Ethereum diamond logo and label 'Ethereum'), then a blue hexagonal Arbitrum-style logo, a black circular logo with two white arrows, a blue-and-white circular logo, a purple circular logo, a light-blue droplet logo, a red circular OP logo, a green Solana-style logo, a red triangular Avalanche-style logo, a yellow circular logo, a black N-shaped logo, a teal striped circular logo, and a yellow box with black C logo at the bottom. Right column of nodes, top to bottom: blue hexagonal logo with value $13B above, Ethereum node (grey box with Ethereum logo and label 'Ethereum', no value printed), black circular logo ($4.9B), blue-and-white circular logo ($3.9B), droplet logo ($2.4B), red OP logo ($3.6B), green Solana logo ($2.3B), purple circular logo ($3.6B), red Avalanche logo, yellow circular logo, black N logo, teal striped logo and yellow C logo, with no values printed for the last five. Left values, top to bottom: Ethereum total labelled '$34B outflows' with an arrow beneath pointing right; blue hexagon $11B; black circle $5.7B; blue circle $2.9B; purple circle $3.6B; droplet $1.4B; OP $1.9B; Solana $1.1B; the remaining left nodes have no printed value. Ribbons: thick ribbons flow from Ethereum on the left (grey, about 3 to 4 grey ribbons) to the right column in a fan; coloured ribbons from each left node (blue, violet, light purple, red, green, yellow) curve up or down to the matching right-column nodes; many faint thin ribbons in pale tints cross the middle. Ribbons are semi-transparent, about 40 to 60% opacity. A dark navy label box reading '$28B inflows' with a short arrow beneath pointing right sits above the right column, with white text. Each node is a rounded-corner-free rectangle with a pastel tinted fill (blue #A9CFFA, lavender #C2B5F5, mint #9EF5C9, salmon #F79A9A, yellow #F5D27A) and a darker inner strip. The a16z wordmark is a grey outlined logo bottom-left. No photographs.

**Design**

Background is white below a header band. Header band has a left-to-right gradient from pale mint #CDEFE0 through near-white #EEF8F4 to pale blue-grey #C9E3EC. The word 'multichain' sits on a lime highlight block #E2FA6B. Title text is bold humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), navy #12335A, about 4.9% of slide height per line, sized so the cap height is about 3.5% of slide height. Subtitle is Noto Sans Bold, navy #12335A, about 2.5% of slide height. Chart value labels are Noto Sans Medium, navy #12335A, about 1.7%. Node labels 'Ethereum' are Noto Sans Bold navy #12335A about 2.0%. Flow labels '$34B outflows' and '$28B inflows' are Noto Sans Bold white text on navy #12335A label boxes. Footer copyright is Noto Sans Medium #D9D9D9 about 1.2%; footnote and source text are Noto Sans Regular #595959 about 1.4%; the footnote link 'a16z.com/investment-list' is underlined. Page number is Noto Sans Medium #999999 about 1.5%. Thin rule under the header is a gradient from yellow #F5D547 at the left to green #3C8D5A at the right, about 0.4% of height. No shadows, no corner radii on the header.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Review: Typography, colour-coded highlights and chart treatment are consistent, and slides 10, 33 and 23 read cleanly at size.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A narrow left panel (about 0 to 30% of the width) holds a large left-aligned bold title stacked in eight lines, starting near 6.8% from the top and ending near 59% of the height, with a margin of about 3.3% from the left edge. The right area (about 36 to 96% of the width) holds a chart subtitle at top (y 7.1%), the chart block from y 14.7% to 81%, a callout text block inside the chart's upper-left, and a footnote and source line at the bottom (y 92.6 to 97.7%). The page number 23 sits bottom right at x 97.8%, and the a16z crypto wordmark and copyright line sit bottom left over the panel.

**Visuals**

One chart, a combined area and line chart titled 'Fees paid by L2s on Ethereum (% of all L1 fees, rolling 30d avg)' with superscript 1, subtitled 'before and after EIP-4844'. Left y-axis labelled in ETH units from 0 to 20M, with tick labels Ξ 0, Ξ 5M, Ξ 10M, Ξ 15M, Ξ 20M, in navy label boxes with white text. Right y-axis in percent from 0% to 20%, with tick labels 0%, 5%, 10%, 15%, 20% in pale cyan label boxes with navy text. X-axis years 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 in grey, spanning roughly mid-2020 to late 2024. Horizontal gridlines in light grey at each left-axis tick. Series 1, a filled light teal area (fees paid by L2s as % of L1 fees, right axis) that rises from near 0% in 2020 to a peak of about 12.5% in early 2023, a drop to about 10% then about 13% through 2023, a sharp peak of about 17% in mid-2024 with values near 10 to 15% through late 2024, and then a steep fall after March 2024 to about 1% by late 2024. Series 2, a thin dark navy line (ETH-denominated value on L2s, left axis) that rises from about 0 in 2020 to about 5M ETH by early 2023 and about 10M to 15M ETH through 2024; values are estimates marked about. A vertical divider at the March 2024 upgrade splits the chart into Before and After regions; the After region is shaded with a translucent teal tint. Arrows under the words Before and After are thin navy lines pointing left and right respectively. An annotation label reading ETH-denominated value on L2s² in white on a navy box sits above the right end, with a thin navy leader line dropping to the dark navy line near 2024. Highlighted points: none marked beyond the shaded After region and the annotation. Legend: none. The chart is not cropped and sits in an unboxed white area.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left panel mint-to-yellow gradient, right area solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate editorial style on white. Left panel uses a soft gradient from mint green #C4EEDD at top-left through pale #E6F7EE to pale yellow #FDF8D5 at bottom. A vertical divider stripe runs along the panel's right edge, graded from teal #00958B at top to gold #C9A800 at bottom. Title text is navy #12335A in NotoSans Bold, about 4.9% of slide height, with the word 'reducing costs' highlighted by a lime rectangle #E2FF6B. Chart subtitle is navy #12335A NotoSans Bold about 2.5% of height. Callout body text is navy #12335A NotoSans Medium about 3.7% of height, with 'proto-danksharding' in Bold. Chart labels are small (about 1.6% of height). Footnote and sources are grey #595959 NotoSans Regular about 1.4%. Page number is #999999. Copyright is pale grey #D9D9D9. Accent colours are teal #00958B, cyan area #7FDDE0, dark navy line #1B5A73. Shapes have square corners, no shadows, no rounded corners.

24
Ethereum scaling efforts have slashed onchain transaction costs by over 99%

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a 16:9 slide. The left area (about 0 to 75 percent of width) holds a vertical timeline of four stacked pill-shaped rows with lime dots running down the left edge, with the title at top left and a thin navy rule to its right. A narrow vertical gradient band at about 70 percent of width separates a right-hand column (about 70 to 100 percent) holding a large bold headline set flush left at top. Footnotes sit bottom-centre of the left area, the a16z logo sits bottom-left, the copyright line sits bottom-left below it, and the page number 24 sits bottom-right at about 97.8 percent x and 96 percent y.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a vertical cost ladder of four stacked horizontal pill-shaped rows, each row a rounded rectangle with a fully rounded left and right end, connected by short vertical teal lines. Row 1 (top) is a pill with a dashed light-grey outline containing the value about $44 in bold teal and the label via international wire transfer. Row 2 is a pill with a thin solid grey outline containing $12 in bold teal and the label via USDC on Ethereum (2021 avg gas price), with 2021 underlined. Row 3 is a pill with a thin solid grey outline containing $1 in bold teal and the label via USDC on Ethereum (Sept 2024 avg gas price), with Sept 2024 underlined. Row 4 (bottom) is a pill with a thin solid grey outline containing <$0.01 in bold teal and the label via USDC on Base L2 (Sept 2024 avg gas price), with Sept 2024 underlined. Each row's value is a large bold teal number (#24C5C9) and each label is regular dark grey text. Connectors: short teal vertical lines (#24C5C9, about 1 px) join the bottom of each pill to the top of the next, centred horizontally at about 37 percent of slide width. A column of eleven lime-green filled circles (about 1.7 percent of width each) runs down the left edge at about 7 percent x, overlapping the left ends of the pills. A lime-green downward-pointing triangle sits at the left end of the bottom row. No photographs, charts with values, or logos other than the a16z wordmark. Values are printed on the slide, so no estimates are needed.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the left, with a mint-to-pale-yellow gradient panel (#C6EFE2 to #FFF9CF) on the right 30 percent of the slide. White background on the left area; right column has a soft gradient from mint #C6EFE2 at top to pale yellow #FFF9CF at bottom with a teal-to-gold vertical gradient strip (#2BAE93 top to #D4A800 bottom, about 0.5 percent of width) at its left edge. Title text is bold sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold) in navy #12335A, about 3.2 percent of slide height, with the word Cost highlighted by a lime #E2FF5C rectangle. The right headline is Noto Sans Bold in navy #12335A at about 4.9 percent of slide height, with the words over and 99% highlighted by lime #E2FF5C rectangles. Values are Noto Sans Bold in teal #24C5C9 at about 6.0 percent of slide height. Labels and footnotes are Noto Sans Regular in grey #595959 at about 2.8 and 1.4 percent. Copyright line is Noto Sans Medium in light grey #D9D9D9 at about 1.2 percent; page number Noto Sans Medium #999999 at about 1.5 percent. Accent colours are lime #E2FF5C for dots, triangle and highlights, and navy #12335A for the title rule. Pills have 1 px grey #A6A6A6 outlines and fully rounded ends; the top pill is dashed light grey #BFBFBF. No shadows or gradients on the pills.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a full-width title band across the top, the title set left-aligned in two lines starting at about 3.5% from the left edge. Below a thin yellow-to-green rule at about 21% height, the chart subtitle sits left-aligned at 3.4% and a wide stacked area chart fills the middle, spanning about 6% to 96% of the width. A footer row at the bottom holds the a16z logo at bottom left with copyright line beneath it, two footnote blocks in the centre-left and centre-right, and the page number 25 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked area chart with a combined line overlay, titled 'Total value locked in non-EVM blockchains¹' with a superscript 1 footnote marker. Left y-axis labelled in US dollars from $0 to $15B with gridlines at $5B, $10B and $15B; the plot rises to about $17B at the right edge, the top of the range being unlabelled. Right y-axis labelled 0, 2.5M, 5.0M and 7.5M, with a grey callout box reading 9.4 million at the top right end of the line. X-axis labels 2023 (left edge) and 2024 (about one-third of the way from the right), with light grey vertical gridlines at about the 2023 mid-year, at 2024 and at about late 2024. Series stacked from bottom to top: Solana (purple, about $12-13B by the end of the period, about $1B at start of 2023), Sui (light cyan, about $1-2B at the end), Aptos (dark teal, about $1-2B at the end), TON (light orange, about $1-2B at the end) and NEAR (light green, top band). Total stack peaks near $17B at the far right and shows a sharp dip to about $9B in late 2024 for Solana. Combined daily active addresses is a dark grey line (about 1.5M at start, about 2.5M in mid-2023, about 4.3M at start of 2024, about 6M at late 2024 peak, climbing to 9.4 million in the last point) on the right axis. Legend at upper left: NEAR (green square), TON (orange square), Aptos (dark teal square), Sui (light cyan square), Solana (purple square), listed top to bottom, plus a dark line swatch labelled Combined daily active addresses² to the right of NEAR. No values are printed on the series other than the 9.4 million callout. Logo: a16z crypto wordmark in outlined grey lettering at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pale gradient header band from mint #C6EEE0 at left to light blue-grey #C5DCE3 at right across the top. White content area with a pale mint-to-white-to-blue-grey gradient header band across the top, separated from the body by a thin horizontal rule that shifts from yellow to green to dark green. Title text is dark navy and bold in a humanist sans-serif similar to Noto Sans Bold, about 4.9% of slide height per line, with line spacing about 7%. Subtitle is bold navy sans at about 2.5% of slide height. Chart and footnote text is grey sans-serif (Noto Sans Medium/Regular) at about 1.4% to 2.1% of slide height. Accent colours: navy #12335A for titles and legend text, purple #6A3A7E for Solana, light cyan #8EEEF8 for Sui, dark teal #1B7A5E for Aptos, orange #FFB960 for TON, green #7AB648 for NEAR. Gridlines are thin light grey #BFBFBF, chart axis labels grey #595959, copyright text very light grey #D9D9D9. No shadows or rounded corners; no photographs.

### Slide 26

**Text**

Blockchains now process 50x as many transactions per second as / they did just four years ago / Number of blockchain transactions processed per second / Bitcoin / Ethereum L1 / Tron / Ethereum L2s / Solana / January 1st, 2020 / September 30th, 2024 / 1 Second / 1 Second / 26 / One dot represents one transaction. / Some of the above are a16z investments. For a full list see a16z.com/investment-list. This content should not be considered investment advice. / Sources: L2Beat and Chainspect as of 9/30/2024. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top about 21% of the slide height holds a two-line bold title left-aligned at x 3.5% with a thin gold-to-green rule beneath it. Below, a legend row sits at about 26% height with its label at left and four-plus-one hexagon swatches to the right. The main area is a row of hexagon-dot columns: a small January 1st 2020 cluster at far left, a tall vertical navy rule at x 22.4%, and a large September 30th 2024 grid spanning x 30% to 90%, with edge fade columns at both sides. Footer runs along the bottom: a16z logo and copyright at left, a centred disclaimer, and a right-aligned sources note with page number 26 at far right.

**Visuals**

Hexagon dot chart (a unit-of-measure diagram, not a conventional axis chart). Each hexagon is one blockchain transaction processed in one second. Title: Number of blockchain transactions processed per second. Legend in header row, left to right: dark rust hexagon Bitcoin, lime-green hexagon Ethereum L1, orange hexagon Tron, teal hexagon Ethereum L2s, lavender-purple hexagon Solana. Left panel labelled January 1st, 2020: three small faded clusters of hexagons (about 1 second each) with the dots in the same colours but very pale; the middle cluster is the most saturated, showing a few rust, lime and orange hexagons. A bracket-style label 1 Second sits below it. Right panel labelled September 30th, 2024: one dense grid of about 1 second of transactions, arranged in vertical colour bands from left to right: a column of about 8 rust Bitcoin hexagons at top then lime Ethereum L1 hexagons below, then an orange Tron band about 4 hexagons wide, a teal Ethereum L2s band about 13 hexagons wide, and a lavender Solana block filling the rest to the right edge. A thin navy horizontal line with end ticks crosses the grid at about 74% height, labelled 1 Second in a white-backed box. Faded edge columns of pale hexagons at x 90.9% (rust, orange, pale green, pale teal tints) and at far left of the 2020 panel show the continuing grid. Hexagons are flat-top with white gaps; no axes, no units beyond per second.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pastel mint-to-pale-blue gradient header band (#CDEFE2 at left to #C4DDE6 at right) across the top about 21% of the slide.
