**Layout**

Two-column slide: a narrow left panel about 30% of the width holds the title, a sub-heading and a bulleted list, separated by a thin vertical gradient rule at about x 30%; the right area holds a chart title at top, a stacked column chart spanning about x 39% to 88% and y 8% to 77%, with a footnote block and source lines below. Title is top-left at x 3.3%, y 6.8%, left-aligned; the left margin is about 3.3% and the right margin about 2.5%. The logo sits bottom-left with the copyright line beneath it; the page number 27 sits bottom-right at x 97.8%, y 96.2%.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: Monthly user operations related to account abstraction (ERC-4337)¹. Type: stacked vertical columns, one column per month, from about Mar ‘23 to Sep ‘24. Y axis: user operations (no unit printed, read as millions), linear 0 to 20M with labelled ticks at 0, 5M, 10M, 15M and 20M, right-aligned; horizontal light grey gridlines at 5M, 10M, 15M and 20M and a baseline at 0. X axis: month labels Mar ‘23, Jun ‘23, Sep ‘23, Dec ‘23, Mar ‘23 (printed twice as shown), Jun ‘24, Sep ‘24. Series, stacked bottom to top in legend order reversed: Gnosis (light cyan #8EEBF8), Ethereum (lilac #C77DDB), Avalanche C-Chain (turquoise #5CCFD6), Celo (yellow #FCD60A), BNB Chain (orange #FF6A00), Optimism (pink #FF3D98), Arbitrum (green #6EB33F), Polygon (purple #6B3585), Base (dark teal #17607A) on top. No values are printed on the bars; estimates: the last five columns are about 4.1M, 4.2M, 15.5M, 18.2M and 13.3M in total, with Polygon about 2.4M to 2.5M in the Jun ‘24 to Sep ‘24 columns and Base about 11.5M, 14M and 9.2M in the three most recent columns; earlier columns are below about 3M, with Polygon the dominant segment. Legend: vertical list at top left of plot with square swatches in the nine series colours, listed Base, Polygon, Arbitrum, Optimism, BNB Chain, Celo, Avalanche C-Chain, Ethereum, Gnosis. Highlight: a thin dark bracket spans the last three columns from about 18.7% to 60.7% of slide height with a leader line at y 37.4% pointing to the annotation text. No highlighted points beyond the bracket. Chart image is a flat vector graphic with no shadow.

**Design**

Background: Left panel: pastel mint-to-pale-yellow gradient (#C4EBDD to #FBF8D0); right area: solid white #FFFFFF. Left panel uses a soft pastel gradient from mint green #C4EBDD at top left through pale mint #DDF5E8 to pale yellow #FBF8D0 at bottom; the right area is plain white #FFFFFF. Titles and body text are dark navy #12335A. Accents are teal bullets #24C5C9 and highlight blocks #E2FF6B (lime yellow) behind two title lines; the divider is a vertical gradient from teal #1FA88A to gold #C9A800. Typeface: Noto Sans (humanist sans-serif), Bold for title and chart annotation, SemiBold for sub-heading, Regular for bullets and notes, Medium for axis labels; title text is about 4.9% of slide height, sub-heading about 3.3%, bullets and chart title about 2.2%, footnotes about 1.4%. Lines are thin dark grey #595959 or light grey gridlines, square-cornered; no shadows or corner radii.

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top with the two-line title left-aligned at about 3.5% margin. Below it, a left column (about 3% to 63% width) holds the bar-and-line chart with its subtitle above, and a right column (about 70% to 97% width) holds a large statement and the footnotes; the footer runs along the bottom with the a16z logo at bottom-left, the centred footnote at about 29% x, the sources at about 70.5% x, and the page number 28 at bottom-right (about 97.8% x, 96.2% y).

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column plus line chart titled 'Amount spent monthly to verify ZK proofs on Ethereum¹ before and after EIP-4844'. Left y-axis labelled in US dollars with ticks $0, $5M, $10M, $15M (gridlines at each tick, grey #595959 labels). Right y-axis labelled in ETH with ticks Ξ 0, Ξ 500K, Ξ 1M, Ξ 1.5M (white labels on dark navy tags). X-axis has year labels 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 at about 11.4%, 25.0%, 38.5% and 52.0% of slide width; no unit given. Series: columns stacked by project, colours Tornado Cash red-orange #FF3A00, dYdX (StarkEx) dark grey #5A5A5A, Loopring magenta #F0399A, Aztec dark green #004D1A, StarkEx projects (excl. Starknet L2) pink #F7A8C0, Starknet light yellow-green #E2FF6B, Railgun orange #FF7A00, zkSync Era cyan #5CD3DC, Polygon zkEVM pale cyan #8EEEF7, Nocturne teal #1B6A8A, Linea navy #142B4D, Scroll green #6AB04C. Most monthly values are about $0.1M to $1M in 2021 to 2023; the largest columns are about $2M in late 2021, about $4M in late 2023 and about $15M in early 2024 (Linea-dominated, about $0.5M to $1M tall Scroll cap). Dark navy line (ETH-denominated value on ZK rollups, read on the right axis) is about Ξ 0.1M in 2021, peaks about Ξ 0.9M in mid 2022, dips to about Ξ 0.25M in early 2023, rises to about Ξ 0.6M late 2023, and climbs steeply to about Ξ 1.5M+ in 2024 with a spike near the top. Legend: 13 colour swatches with labels stacked vertically at left inside the plot. Highlighted points: 'Before' and 'After' arrows above the chart; the 'After' period is a shaded column band in translucent teal covering the right end of the plot; a white callout label 'ETH-denominated value on ZK rollups²' with leader to the line. Also a small green Scroll-coloured cap on the tallest bar and a small pale-yellow legend box. Chart is boxed with no border; no axis lines except light grey baseline.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pale mint-to-blue gradient header band across the top about 21% of the height. Clean corporate-research style on white. Header band uses a soft horizontal gradient from pale mint #C8EDE2 on the left, through near-white #EEF8F3 in the middle, to pale steel blue #C4D9E3 on the right, with a thin gradient rule beneath it running from yellow #F5D547 at left to green #3E8E5A at right. Title is bold humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), navy #12335A, about 4.9% of slide height, two lines with generous leading. Subtitle and chart title are Noto Sans Bold about 2.5% of height in navy #12335A. Statement on right is Noto Sans Medium about 5.2% of height in navy #12335A with '250' in Noto Sans Bold 6.4% on a lime #E2FF6B highlight block. Footnotes and sources are Noto Sans Regular about 1.4% of height in grey #595959; copyright in Noto Sans Medium #D9D9D9. Accent colours are lime #E2FF6B and teal #00958B at 20% opacity. No rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients other than header.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Herodotus / Axiom / Lagrange / Use cases / Data-intensive applications / Machine learning / Onchain games / Governance / Bridging / Options pricing (Black-Scholes) / Orderbooks / State proofs / Fetch web2 data (zkTLS and zkEmail) / RISC ZERO / Succinct / Jolt / ZK proofs can / unlock a new / programming / paradigm for / blockchains / “ZK coprocessors” allow complex computations to be run offchain, giving smart contract developers access to cheap, verifiable compute. / Zero knowledge is used as a catch-all for cryptography that can succinctly prove that offloaded computations were performed correctly. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide. / 29

**Layout**

Two zones: a left content area about 75% of the slide width holding logos, a light grey band of three bulleted columns and a logo row, and a right panel about 25% wide with a gradient background carrying the headline and quote. The 'Use cases' label sits at the top left of the grey band, left aligned with a margin of about 2.4%. The slide number sits bottom right at about 97.8% x, and the a16z crypto wordmark and copyright sit bottom left, with the footer sentence centred in the bottom strip.

**Visuals**

Logos only, no photographs or charts. Herodotus: black wordmark with a black outlined angular glyph (H-like pattern) above and centred, about x 27.5 to 47.1 percent, y 4.5 to 11.3 percent. Axiom: black geometric triangle-and-A mark followed by black spaced capitals AXIOM, at left, y about 16 to 24 percent. Lagrange: a multicolour ring (red, orange, yellow, teal, pink strokes) followed by navy #0B0B5C capitals LAGRANGE, at right, y about 13 to 25 percent. RISC Zero: solid black vertical rectangle beside an outlined square with a diagonal line, with black spaced text RISC above ZERO below. Succinct: black stylised S-shaped mark with grey gradient streaks and black lowercase wordmark Succinct. Jolt: blocky black letters with orange square dots and a small orange tag reading ALPHA at top right. The a16z crypto wordmark is a pale grey outlined logo at bottom left. Vertical thin grey divider lines (#8A93A6, about 1 px) sit in the grey band at about x 23.2 and 47.6 percent, spanning y 32.9 to 57.3 percent. Right panel: a vertical strip at about x 75.3 percent, width about 0.4 percent, running the full height with a gradient from teal #2BAE95 at top to gold #C9A227 at bottom. The panel fill is a diagonal gradient from mint #C6EFE0 at top left through pale green #E3F7EC to pale yellow #FBF9CF at bottom right. Cropping: logo images are placed without visible boxes on white. Highlight: yellow-green #E2FF6B rectangles sit behind 'Use cases' and 'cheap, verifiable'.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the left area, with a mint-to-pale-yellow gradient panel (#C6EFE0 to #FBF9CF) on the right 25 percent. Left area white #FFFFFF with a light grey band #F9F9F6 behind the three columns. Headline and quote text navy #12335A. Body bullets navy #12335A. Bullet dots teal #24C5C9. Footer sentence grey #595959. Copyright grey #D9D9D9. Slide number grey #999999. Typefaces: Noto Sans. Headline Noto Sans Bold, about 4.9 percent of slide height, sentence case. Quote Noto Sans SemiBold, about 3.3 percent. Column bullets Noto Sans Regular, about 3.0 percent, with generous line spacing (about 6 percent of height between wrapped lines). 'Use cases' Noto Sans Bold, about 2.7 percent. Footer about 1.4 percent. Lines are thin grey #8A93A6 dividers. No rounded corners, shadows or other effects.

### Slide 30

**Text**

Zero knowledge / virtual / machines / (zkVMs) are / improving, but / there is still a / long way to go
Intel CPU clock speed in MHz (log scale)¹
State of the art zkVMs²
10,000

zkVMs allow developers to write programs in high-level, programmer-friendly languages like Rust or Go / without requiring any knowledge of the underlying math that proves their execution. But the improved / usability typically adds significant performance overhead.
Sources: 1/ Intel, Wikipedia, K. Olukoton (published by Bruno Carpentieri in 2015) 2/ a16z crypto analysis as of 9/30/2024.
30
©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-part slide: a narrow left panel (about 0 to 30 percent of the width) holds the bold title stacked in six lines starting near 7 percent from the top, with the a16z logo and copyright at the bottom left. The right area (about 33 to 98 percent) holds a chart title at the top, a scatter chart with an inset callout above a full-width rounded text box below it, and a source line bottom right with the page number at the far right; left margin about 3 percent and right margin about 2 percent.

**Visuals**

Chart: scatter plot titled 'Intel CPU clock speed in MHz (log scale)¹'. Y axis is logarithmic with labelled ticks 1, 10, 100, 1,000 and 10,000 (MHz); no axis title. X axis shows years 1970 to 2005 at five-year ticks (1970, 1975, 1980, 1985, 1990, 1995, 2000, 2005), with the axis continuing slightly past 2005. Vertical and horizontal thin navy-grey gridlines. A light grey band (#EBEBEB at about 61 percent opacity) covers the area left of 1970. Purple #5E2C7E dots, about 130 points in total: about 1971 to 1975 near 1 to 2 MHz, sparse points around 1976 to 1982 near 5 to 6 MHz, a cluster of points from 1985 to 1995 between about 15 and 60 MHz, and a dense cluster from 1996 to 2002 between about 100 and 2,000 MHz, with a few points near 3,000 to 4,000 MHz around 2003 to 2005. No individual values are printed, so values are estimates. A thin diagonal grey trend line runs from the origin box at the bottom left to the top right near 2005 at about 4,000 MHz. A small dotted rounded box at the lower left of the chart (about x 53 to 56 percent, y 64 to 69 percent) surrounds an orange dot and a green dot at the origin of the chart. Inset callout: dotted rounded rectangle containing a small two-point chart titled 'State of the art zkVMs²', with a vertical axis labelled 0.1 (upper) and 0.01 (lower) and a horizontal baseline with labels 2022 and 2024. An orange #FF7300 filled circle (about 2.5 by 4.4 percent) sits at the 2022 position near 0.01 and a green #70B840 filled circle sits at the 2024 position near 0.1. A navy arrow (#12335A, about 1 pt) runs diagonally from the orange circle to the green circle, labelled '10x'. A grey gradient wedge connects the inset box bottom-right to the small dotted box on the chart origin, showing the zoom. Logo: a16z crypto wordmark 'al6zcrypto' in outlined light grey lettering at the bottom left. Lighting: flat, no photography. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Split background: left panel soft mint-to-pale-yellow diagonal gradient (about #BDEBDD to #FBF8D0) and right area solid white #FFFFFF. Left panel background is a soft diagonal gradient from mint #BDEBDD at the top left through pale green #E3F5EA to pale yellow #FBF8D0 at the bottom. A thin vertical gradient stripe at the panel edge runs from teal #1FA58A at top through yellow-green to gold #C8A400 at the bottom. Right area is plain white #FFFFFF. Title text is bold NotoSans-Bold (humanist sans-serif), navy #12335A, about 4.9 percent of slide height per line (about 7 percent for capitals). Body text in the rounded box is NotoSans-Regular, navy #13335A, about 2.35 percent of slide height. Chart title is NotoSans-Bold navy #12335A about 2.5 percent. Axis labels are NotoSans-Medium grey #595959 about 1.6 percent. Inset title is NotoSans-Bold #13335A about 2.0 percent. Accent colours: purple #5E2C7E dots, orange #FF7300, green #70B840, navy arrow #12335A. Shapes: the body text box is a rounded rectangle with a thin light grey #A6A6A6 outline about 0.75 pt and a corner radius of about 2 percent of width, no fill, no shadow. The inset is a rounded rectangle with a dark grey #404040 dotted outline about 1 pt and large corner radius of about 4 percent of width, white fill. Source line NotoSans-Regular grey #595959 about 1.36 percent. Copyright NotoSans-Medium #D9D9D9 about 1.23 percent. Page number NotoSans-Medium #999999 about 1.48 percent.

31

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top (about the top 22% of the slide) holding a two-line bold title left-aligned at a left margin of about 3.5%, with a thin gradient rule beneath it. Below, three equal-width 3D pie charts sit in three columns, each with a bold heading and a thin grey hairline above its date label; arrows run between the first and second and second and third charts. The legend sits bottom right, the a16z logo and copyright bottom left, the footnote centred-left at about 27%, the sources line bottom right and the page number at the far bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Three 3D pie charts (no axis, no title beyond column headings), each shown as a tilted cylinder with a flat top ellipse and darker teal side wall. Chart 1, Pre-Merge, Sept 2022: slices Staked 11% (deep purple, about 40 degrees, from 12 o'clock clockwise) and Unstaked 89% (light cyan). Chart 2, 1 year post-Merge, Sept 2023: Staked 22% (deep purple, slice from 12 o'clock clockwise to about 3 o'clock), Unstaked 78% (light cyan), and a very thin pale purple sliver just left of 12 o'clock with no printed value (about 1%, unlabelled, Eigenlayer colour). Chart 3, 2 years post-Merge, Sept 2024: Staked 29% (deep purple, from about 12 o'clock to about 4 o'clock, pulled slightly outward), Unstaked 71% (light cyan), and a raised exploded group at about 10 to 12 o'clock made of three re-staking slices totalling 5% with a callout label '(Re-staked 5%)': Eigenlayer light purple (widest), Symbiotic hot pink (middle), Karak light pink (thin, next to the staked slice); the callout has a magenta box with white bold text and an elbow leader line running from the exploded slices to the label at the top right. Two black arrow symbols, each made of two solid black dots followed by a solid black right-pointing triangle, sit between charts 1 and 2 and between charts 2 and 3 at mid-height of the charts. Legend at bottom right: three small solid squares, purple-lilac #C77DD6 for Eigenlayer, hot pink #FF2E9A for Symbiotic, light pink #F7A8D0 for Karak. Logo: the a16z crypto wordmark, outlined light grey lettering, bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a mint-to-light-blue gradient header band across the top about 22% of the slide. White #FFFFFF background below a header band with a mint-to-pale-blue gradient (mint #C8EFE0 at left, pale green #EAF8EC near centre, light blue-grey #C5DDE3 at right). Thin rule under the header runs yellow #F5D547 at left to green #3D8B5E at right. Title and headings are navy #12335A in Noto Sans Bold (sans-serif, bold); title cap height about 4.9% of slide height, body/labels about 1.4% to 2.5%. Chart labels are white bold on purple and navy #13335A bold on cyan. Staked purple #5E3278, Unstaked cyan #7ACFD6, chart side walls dark teal #235A5E. Footnote and sources text in grey #595959, copyright in light grey #D9D9D9, page number in grey #999999. Hairline dividers are light grey #D9D9D9 about 0.1% thick. Flat shapes with no rounded corners and no shadows except the subtle 3D shading on the pie cylinders.

**Layout**

Full-bleed slide with a pixelated gradient band across the middle. Left-aligned kicker and two-line headline sit in the upper-middle band with a left margin of about 2.5%; a single row of nine category labels is centred across the lower band; the logo and copyright line sit bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A decorative background pattern rather than a photograph: a blurred pink-to-violet gradient at the top, overlaid on a middle band of large square mosaic tiles that shift from pink (#F76FA0 approx.) at left to violet (#8E2A9E approx.) at right, and from magenta to deep purple (#4A1070 approx.) toward the bottom. Lower band is a dark magenta-to-indigo gradient (#8A1A7A to #3A1468 approx.). Tiles are unbordered and slightly translucent. Small a16z crypto wordmark logo in white at bottom-left. No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: full-bleed pixel-mosaic gradient in pink, magenta and purple. Titles: white (#FFFFFF) Noto Sans ExtraBold, sans-serif, about 14.3% of slide height cap-to-line box, two lines, tight leading. Kicker: Noto Sans Medium, dark navy #12335A, about 3.0% of slide height. Category labels: white Noto Sans Regular, about 2.1% of slide height. Copyright: Noto Sans Medium, light grey #D9D9D9, about 1.2%. Page number: Noto Sans Medium, grey #999999, about 1.5%. No shapes, lines, corner radii or shadows.

### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A simple category breakdown uses sized blocks with direct labels and percentages, making the share of each use case immediately comparable.
- Review: Typography, colour-coded highlights and chart treatment are consistent, and slides 10, 33 and 23 read cleanly at size.

**Text**

DeFi and stablecoins dominate crypto usage today — emerging / categories include gaming and social / Category breakdown by daily active addresses (all blockchains) / Decentralized finance (DeFi) / 34% / Stablecoins / 32% / Infra (bridges, / oracles, MEV, / account / abstraction, etc.) / 14% / Token / transfers / 13% / Centralized / exchanges 3% / Gaming / 2% / NFTs / 1% / Social / <1% / Active addresses can be associated with more than one category. / Source: Artemis as of 9/30/2024, excludes wallet-to-wallet transfers. / 33 / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

A single full-width treemap-style block chart sits below a two-line title and a subtitle, with a left margin of about 3.5% and a footer band at the bottom. The title spans the top-left in two lines above a full-width gradient rule at about 21% of slide height; the subtitle is left-aligned at about 27.9%. The rectangles fill x 3.8% to about 92.6% and y 34.5% to 88.5%, with the large DeFi and Stablecoins tiles on the left and a column of Infra and Token transfers above a row of smaller tiles on the right. The alózcrypto logo sits bottom-left, the copyright line beneath it, the source notes bottom-right, and the page number '33' at the far bottom-right (about 97.8%).

**Visuals**

Treemap chart with no axes, legend or gridlines; each tile is a filled rectangle with its category label and share printed inside the top-left corner. Chart subtitle: 'Category breakdown by daily active addresses (all blockchains)'. Tiles and values: Decentralized finance (DeFi) 34% in dark green #197152 (large, left, about 28.5% wide by 53.9% tall); Stablecoins 32% in turquoise #24C5C9 (large, about 28.6% wide by 53.9% tall); Infra (bridges, oracles, MEV, account abstraction, etc.) 14% in pink #FDA5CB (about 15.3% wide by 43.6% tall); Token transfers 13% in lime #E2FA6B (about 14.9% wide by 43.6% tall); Centralized exchanges 3% in orange #F97101 (about 13.2% wide by 9.4% tall, bottom row); Gaming 2% in dark teal #18607C (about 8.2% wide by 9.4% tall); NFTs 1% in green #6FB540 (about 6.4% wide by 9.5% tall); Social <1% in pale cyan about #A0E1E8 (a narrow tile about 1.6% wide, unlabelled inside, with its label set beside it at the right edge and highlighted by a pale cyan band). Tile sizes are proportional to share. Gaps of about 0.5% separate tiles. No highlighted points beyond the Social highlight band. The alózcrypto logo is an outlined grey wordmark. No photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band across the top 21% of the slide. White content area with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band across the top; a thin horizontal gradient rule from hot pink #F76A9E on the left to purple #8E3BB0 on the right sits under the header at about 21% height. Titles in dark navy #12335A, Noto Sans Bold (humanist sans-serif), title text about 4.9% of slide height (cap height about 3.5%). Subtitle and tile labels also Noto Sans Bold in navy #13335A, about 2.5% to 3.0% of slide height. Tile label text is white on the dark green and orange and darker tiles and navy on the light tiles. Footer caption Noto Sans Regular in grey #595959 about 1.4% of height; copyright in light grey #D9D9D9 Noto Sans Medium about 1.2%; page number Noto Sans Medium #999999 about 1.5%. Tiles are flat with square corners, no shadows, no borders.

34

**Layout**

Two-column top area with two stacked bar charts side by side above a full-width table, with a narrow vertical gradient rule and a right-hand panel holding the headline. Chart titles sit top-left of each chart at about 4.6% from the top, the headline sits top-aligned in the right panel from about 6.8% down, and the left margin is about 3.6%. Footnotes are bottom-right of the content area, the a16z logo and copyright are bottom-left, and the page number 34 is bottom-right at about 97.8% x and 96.2% y.

**Visuals**

Two bar charts, a table and a logo. Chart 1 (left): stacked column chart titled Stablecoin supply by issuer with superscript 1. Y-axis in US dollars billions with labels $0, $50B, $100B, $150B and faint horizontal gridlines at $50B, $100B and $150B. X-axis years 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 with one column per month. Series from bottom: BUSD pink, USDC navy, USDT cyan, USDe light orange, Dai purple, Other grey. Total rises from near $0 in 2020 to about $165B around early-mid 2022, dips to about $115B in 2023, then climbs to about $165B by late 2024. USDT is the largest series in most later months, about $110B to $115B by 2024; USDC peaked near $70B to $75B around 2022. No printed data values beyond axis labels. Legend sits inside top-left of plot with six entries as listed in text. Chart 2 (right): stacked column chart titled Adjusted transfer volume with superscript 1 by blockchain. Y-axis labels $0, $500B, $1T with gridlines at $500B and $1T. X-axis years 2020 to 2024. Series from bottom: Ethereum dark teal, Base cyan, BNB Chain orange, Tron red-orange, Solana pink, Other grey, Arbitrum purple at the base. Monthly columns are irregular; the largest spikes reach about $1.2T in mid-2021 and about $1.3T in late 2024, with Tron the largest top segment in 2024. Legend sits top-left inside the plot. Table: 4 columns (blank label column, Transaction volume ($), # of transactions) by 6 rows. Header row: blank, Transaction volume ($), # of transactions in white on purple. Row 1 Stablecoins: $8.5 tn and 1.1 bn in bold black on light mauve. Row 2 PayPal: $0.42 tn and 6.6 bn. Row 3 Visa: $3.96 tn and 59.3 bn. Row 4 ACH: $21.6 tn and 8.3 bn. Row 5 Fedwire: $284.2 tn and 0.05 bn. Value columns in rows 2 to 5 have a very light grey fill. Logo: a16z crypto wordmark in light grey at bottom-left. Right panel has a large background gradient image and magenta highlight bars behind product and market fit.

**Design**

Background: Pale white-to-pink gradient background (#FFFFFF to #F8E4F0) across the slide, with a lilac-to-pink gradient right panel. White and pale pink-to-lilac gradient background. Headline Stablecoins have found in dark navy #12335A bold sans-serif, with product and market fit in white on magenta #E032E0 highlight boxes. Chart titles and table caption in navy #12335A bold sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold) at about 2.5% of slide height. Body and footnote text in grey #595959 regular sans-serif at about 1.4% of slide height. Table header purple #65367D with white regular text; Stablecoins row #B5A0BF; body rows white with #F3F3F3 middle column and thin light grey rules. Vertical rule at the right edge of the content area is a purple-to-pink gradient from #7B2D9B to #F06292. Square corners, no shadows. Copyright line in light grey #D9D9D9.

35
©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A narrow left panel (about 0 to 29% of width) holds the title stacked in six to eight lines, top-left aligned at about 3.3% from the left edge and starting 6.8% from the top. The right area (about 30 to 100% width) holds a chart subtitle at about 10% from the top, with the chart plotting area from about 20% to 88% of height. A source line sits bottom-right and the slide number sits at the bottom-right corner. The copyright line and the alózcrypto logo sit bottom-left on the panel. Left margin about 3.3%, right margin about 2%.

**Visuals**

Chart: combined bar and line chart. Title: none as a separate heading; subtitle reads 'Spot crypto trading volume vs. stablecoin monthly sending addresses'. Left y-axis labelled in US dollars from $0 to $3T with gridlines at $1T, $2T and $3T. Right y-axis from 0 to 30M with labels 10M, 20M and 30M (unit: addresses). X-axis years 2017 to 2024 with one tick per year, monthly data points. Series 1: light cyan bars (#91E0EB) of monthly spot crypto trading volume, read against the left axis; approximate peaks about $3.1T around mid-2021, about $2.8T in early 2021 and about $2.9T in mid-2021, about $2.2T in early 2020 and about $2.2T in mid-2020; the bars are near zero before 2017 to 2019. Series 2: dark navy (#13335A) line of stablecoin monthly sending addresses, read against the right axis; approximate peak about 26M in early 2024 (line near 26M at 2024 Q1), about 22M mid-2024, about 18M in late 2022, about 9M near mid-2021 and about 1M in 2017. No legend; the series are identified by the subtitle colours (cyan highlight for trading volume, grey highlight for addresses). No points are highlighted with markers. The magenta highlight behind the word 'adoption' in the title is a text highlight, not a chart element. Values are estimates marked 'about' because the slide prints no data labels.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF across the right area, with the pink-to-lavender gradient panel covering the left portion. Left panel uses a soft pink-to-lavender gradient from top #FDEEF5 to bottom #EBD3EA, with a thin vertical accent stripe at the panel's right edge (about x 29.0%, 0.4% wide) running from pink #F0508C at top through magenta to deep purple #5B1F9E at bottom. Right area is white #FFFFFF. Title text is bold NotoSans-Bold sans-serif in navy #12335A at about 4.9% of slide height per line with generous line spacing (about 6.8% pitch). The word 'adoption' is white #FFFFFF on a magenta #E032E0 highlight box. Subtitle is NotoSans-Bold about 2.5% of height; 'Spot crypto trading volume' in navy #12335A on cyan #91E0EB highlight; 'vs.' navy; 'stablecoin monthly sending addresses' in grey #595959 on light grey #C4C4C4 highlight. Axis labels NotoSans-Medium about 1.6% of height, navy #13335A on cyan for left axis labels and grey #595959 on grey #C4C4C4 for right axis labels, with the same highlight boxes. Gridlines are thin grey #BFBFBF horizontal lines; baseline is dark grey. Bars have thin cyan outlines and rounded tops with no shadows.

**Layout**

Two-column slide on a white field: a chart occupies the left three-quarters below a bold chart title that sits top-left at about 7% from the top; a narrow vertical gradient bar separates it from a right-hand panel of large bold headline text in a single left-aligned column starting at x 77.9%. A footer row sits at the bottom with the a16z wordmark at bottom-left, a two-line footnote centred-left at x 24.2%, a source line at x 57.7%, and the page number 36 at bottom-right (x 97.8%, y 96.2%). Left margin about 2% and right margin about 2%.

**Visuals**

Chart: scatter plot with a logarithmic y-axis in USD and a linear regression trend line. Title: 'Cloud compute cost, in 2023 USD, to train frontier AI models over time (log scale)'. Y-axis: log scale, $10 at bottom to $1B at top, ticks at $10, $100, $1,000, $10K, $100K, $1M, $10M, $100M, $1B (each decade). X-axis: years 2016 to 2024, ticks at each year, range about mid-2015 to early 2024. Series: one series of dark blue dots (about 45 points) with labelled values where printed: GNMT about $330K (about 2016.7); AlphaZero about $350K (about 2017.9); GPT-3175B (davinci) about $4.5M (about 2020.4); DALL-E about $250K (about 2020.4); PaLM (540B) about $12M (about 2022.2); GPT-4 about $80M (about 2023.2); Gemini 1.0 Ultra about $200M (about late 2023); Inflection-2 about $11M (about 2023.9). Other dots are unlabelled and their values are about $10K to $30M. Regression line: bright green straight line from about $6K at 2015.5 to about $25M at 2024.3, described as a 2.6x per year growth trend. Callout: green arrow from the text 'Regression mean 2.6x/ year' pointing to the line near about $100K in 2018.6. Legend: none. Highlighted points: none marked beyond the labels. Gridlines: thin light grey vertical and horizontal lines at each year and each decade.

**Design**

Background: plain white on the left and light pink-to-lavender gradient on the right panel. Titles and headline: dark navy #12335A, bold Noto Sans (sans-serif), chart title about 2.5% of slide height, headline about 4.8% of slide height with tight line spacing. Chart labels: navy #12335A bold sans-serif about 2.2% of height; axis ticks grey #595959 medium sans-serif about 1.6%. Accent: magenta #E032E0 highlight block behind 'centralization' with white text; green #6FB540 for the regression line, arrow and callout. Vertical accent bar: purple #7A2A9E at top grading to pink #F26A9B at bottom. Footnotes: grey #595959 regular sans-serif about 1.4%. Copyright: very light grey #D9D9D9 medium sans-serif about 1.2%. Page number: #999999 about 1.5%. Shapes have no corner radius, shadows or borders.

### Slide 37

**Text**

Blockchains can address some of the most pressing challenges / facing the AI industry / 15+ billion images / were created by AI / in a year — more / than 150 years of / photography / Training compute for / frontier AI models / has increased ~4x per / year over the last 10 / years / Trust in AI / companies fell from / 50% to 35% in the / U.S. between 2019 / and today / Frontier models are / trained on trillions of / tokens (data units) / from mostly unpaid / contributors / Authenticity / blockchains can help verify the / authenticity of content / Democratization / blockchains can help broaden / access to compute / Transparency / blockchains can help create open, / user-owned services / Ownership / blockchains can help compensate / IP and data contributors / Sources (left to right): EveryPixel (Aug 2023), Epoch AI (Jun 2024), Edelman Trust Survey (Mar 2024), Meta AI Blog (Apr 2024). / 37 / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

A pale header band spans the top fifth of the slide with the two-line bold title left-aligned at about 3.5% from the left edge, sitting above a full-width gradient rule. Below, four equal columns are separated by three thin vertical navy rules; each column holds a large bold statistic in its upper-middle area and a small purple label with a two-line description anchored near the bottom. The alozcrypto logo sits bottom-left, the copyright line beneath it, the sources line bottom-right, and the page number at the far right of the footer row.

**Visuals**

Three thin vertical navy divider lines, about 0.1% wide, running from about 24% to about 91% of slide height at x about 24.3%, 49.2% and 74.9%. Two magenta highlight boxes (#E032E0) behind the text '15+ billion' and '50% to 35%'; two further magenta highlight boxes sit behind '~4x' and 'trillions', each with white bold text. A horizontal gradient rule at about 21% of slide height, roughly 0.5% tall, running pink #F06292 at left to purple #8E2DA0 at right. A pale gradient header band behind the title, pink-tinted at top left (#FDEBF2) fading to lavender-white (#F7EEF9) at right. The alozcrypto logo at bottom-left, rendered as outlined light grey lettering (about #B8B8B8 outline on white). No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient header band at top. Title text navy #12335A, bold humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), about 4.9% of slide height per line with generous line spacing. Statistic text navy #12335A, Noto Sans Bold, about 3.5% of slide height, with white bold text on magenta #E032E0 highlight boxes. Labels in plum #65367D, Noto Sans Bold, about 2.5% of slide height; descriptions in plum #65367D, Noto Sans Medium, about 2.2%. Source line grey #595959, Noto Sans Regular, about 1.4%. Page number #999999 and copyright #D9D9D9, Noto Sans Medium. Dividers are thin navy lines, square ends, no shadows. Highlight boxes are square-cornered, no shadows.

Two horizontal bar charts. Chart 1 title: Narrative mindshare¹ from crypto-related social media. **Layout**

Two-zone slide: a white chart area occupies the left roughly 64% of the width, with the chart title at top-left (y about 5.5%) above a thin navy rule, and a legend beneath it. A thin vertical purple-to-pink accent bar at about x 75% separates it from a right panel (x 77.9 to 95%) carrying the headline, left-aligned at x 77.9% starting near y 6.8%. Footer runs along the bottom: a16z logo and copyright at bottom-left (x 2 to 10%), a disclaimer centred in the left zone (x 24.6%), sources at x 59.7%, and page number 39 at bottom-right (x 97.8%, y 96.2%).

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart with a line overlay. Title: 'Number of NFT collections with 50+ unique minters, by blockchain and month'. X axis: years 2020 to 2024 labelled at January-style positions, monthly columns from 2020 to late 2024. Left Y axis: USD from $0 to $2B, gridlines at $500M, $1B, $1.5B, $2B. Right Y axis: count of collections from 0 to 10.0K, ticks at 2.5K, 5.0K, 7.5K, 10.0K. Series (stacked columns, bottom to top): Ethereum purple, Optimism red-orange, Arbitrum light cyan, Zora pink, Base dark teal. Values are not printed; about 2021 columns are under 200, about mid-2021 to 2022 Ethereum columns reach about 1.0K to 1.5K collections, about late 2022 to early 2023 totals about 2.0K to 2.2K, and the tallest 2024 column (about late 2024) reaches about 10.0K with Base about 7.3K and Zora about 3.0K. Line series: 'Ethereum secondary market volume in USD (excludes wash trading)' in black, drawn over a light grey filled area (#E6E6E6); annotated peak '$1.9B weekly' about mid-2021 with a small arrow to the spike, and '$26M weekly' annotation at the far right at about the level of the last weeks. Legend at top: five colour squares with labels Ethereum, Optimism, Arbitrum, Zora, Base on one row, and a black line sample with the secondary market label on a second row. No highlighted points beyond the two annotations. Photograph or decoration: none apart from the logo.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the chart side, with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient panel (#FFEAF2 to #F2DDF0) behind the right-hand headline area. Clean corporate report look. Background white on the chart side and a pale pink-to-lavender gradient on the right panel. Headline and chart title in dark navy #12335A. Chart axis and footer text grey #595959; page number #999999; copyright #D9D9D9. Accent colours: purple #5B2C6F for Ethereum, orange-red #FF4500 for Optimism, light cyan #8EF0F8 for Arbitrum, pink #F9A8C4 for Zora, teal #1B5E74 for Base, magenta #E032E0 highlight boxes. Typeface NotoSans family: Bold for headline and chart title, Medium for legend and axis labels, Regular for footer. Headline size about 4.9% of slide height; chart title about 2.5%; legend and axis labels about 2.0% and 1.6%; footer about 1.4%. Thin navy rule under chart title, hairline gradient. Vertical accent bar purple to pink. No corner radii or shadows.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white body: a bar chart occupies the left column (about x 4 to 62%) and a token price table the right column (about x 64 to 98%), both below a full-width title header band. The title is left-aligned at x 3.5% in two lines near the top, with the subtitle of the chart left-aligned at x 2.6% just below the header rule. The a16z crypto logo sits bottom-left at x 2.2%, y 93.6%, with the copyright line beneath it. The left footnote block starts at x 26.7% and the right-aligned source note ends at about x 97%, with the page number '40' at the bottom right (x 97.8%, y 96.2%).

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Number of Solana memecoins created daily in 2024' with no legend. Vertical axis labelled 0, 5K, 10K, 15K, 20K (counts of memecoins per day, range 0 to about 21K), with light grey horizontal gridlines at each labelled value. Horizontal axis shows month labels Feb, Mar, Apr, May, Jun, Jul, Aug, Sep with no printed values per bar. Single series of hot pink (#E8408A approx) bars, one per day, with values about 0 to 1K in early February, rising from about March, reaching about 19K in mid May, mostly 8K to 18K from April to August, peaking at about 21K in early August, and about 16.5K to 17K in late September. A dark navy (#142C47) label box with white bold text '10K+ memecoins launched daily' overlaps the lower part of the bars around May to Jun. Right side: a table image styled as a light grey header row (#E6E6E6) labelled TOKEN in the left column and PRICE in the right column, with a white body and thin light grey row dividers, showing 14 rows as listed in text, each with a small circular token avatar, a Solana or Wormhole badge, and a bold token name followed by the price right-aligned. Thumbnail icons include a dark purple circle, a yellow-green token, and small square token images for dogwifhat, Popcat, Orca, Moo Deng, Vita Inu, Bitcoin Cat, wrapped dog, coby, SoBULL, Pochita, POPE, Zazu and Shark Bee. A small a16z crypto wordmark logo in light grey (#BFBFBF) sits bottom left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF solid background, with a pale pink to lavender gradient (#FFF0F5 to #F5EAF8) confined to the title header band. Clean white content area with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient title header band, bottom edge marked by a thin pink-to-purple gradient rule (pink #FF5A9E at left, purple #7B2FBE at right). Title text is bold navy #12335A in a humanist sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), large at about 4.9% of slide height per line with generous leading. Subtitle is bold navy #12335A about 2.5% of slide height. Chart and table text is grey #595959 Noto Sans Medium about 1.6% of height; footnotes are regular grey #595959 about 1.4%; the copyright is light grey #D9D9D9 about 1.2%; page number is #999999 about 1.5%. Accents: pink #E8408A bars, navy #142C47 label box. Table has square corners and no shadow.

**Text**

Thousands of / DeFi protocols / now control / billions of / dollars — and / leaders are / emerging / Top DeFi categories by total value locked / Liquid staking / Lido / 189 other protocols / Lending / Aave / 451 others / Bridge / WBTC / 94 others / DEXes / Uniswap / 1,446 others / Restaking / Eigenlayer / 10 others / Liquid restaking / ether.fi / 22 others / Collateralized debt / MakerDAO / 156 others / Real world assets / Maker RWA / 64 others / Yield / Pendle / 520 others / Derivatives / Jupiter / 274 others / $0 / $10B / $20B / $30B / $40B / Some of the above are a16z investments. **Layout**

Two-column split slide. A narrow pastel gradient panel fills the left 30% of the slide with the headline stacked in seven left-aligned lines starting near 7% from the top and a left margin of about 3.3%; the headline sits in the upper-left and the lower left is empty apart from the logo and copyright at the bottom. A thin vertical magenta-to-purple accent bar separates the panel from the white right area. The right area holds a single horizontal bar chart with its title at top-left, the chart spanning roughly 35% to 89% of the width. The footnote sits bottom-centre-left at about 36.7% x, the source line bottom-right at about 83.7% x, and the page number at the far bottom-right at 97.8% x, with no header.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart titled 'Top DeFi categories by total value locked'. X axis: total value locked in US dollars, linear, from $0 to $40B, gridlines and labels at $0, $10B, $20B, $30B and $40B; values are not printed, so the bar ends below are estimates marked about. Y axis: ten category labels, top to bottom: Liquid staking, Lending, Bridge, DEXes, Restaking, Liquid restaking, Collateralized debt, Real world assets, Yield, Derivatives. Each bar has two segments: a light lime green segment (#E2F76A approx.) for the named leading protocol and a darker green segment (#76B541 approx.) for the remaining protocols, with the label of the leader and a count in navy text at the end of the lime segment. Series, leader segment end and total bar end (about values): Liquid staking, Lido, lime to about $24B, total about $42B, label '189 other protocols'; Lending, Aave, lime to about $20.5B, total about $32B, label '451 others'; Bridge, WBTC, lime to about $9.5B, total about $27.8B, label '94 others'; DEXes, Uniswap, lime to about $4.8B, total about $18.3B, label '1,446 others'; Restaking, Eigenlayer, lime to about $11B, total about $13.6B, label '10 others'; Liquid restaking, ether.fi, lime to about $5.8B, total about $10.4B, label '22 others'; Collateralized debt, MakerDAO, lime to about $4.9B, total about $7.6B, label '156 others'; Real world assets, Maker RWA, lime to about $1.6B, total about $6.3B, label '64 others'; Yield, Pendle, lime to about $2.2B, total about $5.5B, label '520 others'; Derivatives, Jupiter, lime to about $1.2B, total about $3.6B, label '274 others'. Each leader has a small round protocol logo at the start of its bar: Lido (blue-to-pink droplet), Aave (lavender ghost-like icon), WBTC (orange Bitcoin coin in a grey ring), Uniswap (pink unicorn), Eigenlayer (navy 'L' mark in white circle), ether.fi (grey-blue lattice icon), MakerDAO (teal circle with M), Maker RWA (teal circle with M), Pendle (white circle with black thermometer-like mark), Jupiter (black circle with green concentric lines). No legend. No highlighted points; the magenta highlight is on the headline word DeFi, not the chart. Gridlines are light grey vertical lines at each $10B tick; the zero axis is a dark grey vertical line. Bars are rounded-free rectangles with no shadow.

**Design**

Background: Left 30% pale pink-to-lavender gradient (#F8E8F6 at top to #DCD5E8 at bottom) and right 70% solid white #FFFFFF. White right background with a pale pink-to-lavender gradient panel on the left, a magenta-to-purple vertical accent strip. Headline in heavy bold navy sans-serif (Noto Sans Bold), line spacing about 1.5 times size; body and chart labels in Noto Sans Bold and Regular; the footnote and source in Noto Sans Regular at 1.36% of slide height, grey. Title of the headline about 4.9% of slide height, the chart title is about 3.2% of slide height in bold navy, chart labels about 2% in dark grey and navy bold. Colours: headline navy #12335A; highlight box behind DeFi magenta #E03BD8 with white text; chart title navy #12335A; category labels grey #595959; bar lime #E2F76A; bar dark green #76B541; grid lines light grey #D0D0D0; footnote grey #595959; page number grey #999999; copyright very light grey #D9D9D9. Lines are thin and unshadowed; no rounded corners, no shadows.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a chart occupies the left column on a white field, and a tinted pink panel occupies the right column, separated by a thin vertical gradient rule at about 75% of the width. The chart title sits top-left at about 8% from the top with its left edge at about 6%. The right-hand headline is left-aligned, starting at about 78% from the left and 7% from the top. The source line sits bottom-right of the chart area at about 96% from the top, the copyright and logo sit bottom-left, and the page number sits bottom-right at about 97.8% from the left.

**Visuals**

Line chart rendered as an image (1954 by 1206 px) with the following properties. Title: 'Share of decentralized exchange spot trading volume (%)'. Type: line chart with circular markers on each data point, one series, dark green line. Y axis: percentage from 0% to 15%, labelled 0%, 5%, 10%, 15%, with light grey horizontal gridlines at 5%, 10% and 15% and a darker baseline at 0%. X axis: years labelled 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 with one point roughly every quarter or month across about 2020 to late 2024. Approximate values (about): early 2020 near 0%; about 0.9% in mid-2020; about 1.9% late 2020; about 5.5% at the 2020/2021 turn; about 2.6% in early 2021; about 3.6% to 4% in spring 2021; about 6.5% mid-2021; about 8.1% late 2021; about 6% late 2021; about 10.1% early 2022; about 11% in early 2022; about 11.3% in spring 2022; about 9.1% mid-2022; about 10.3% in mid-2022; about 7.5% in late 2022; about 8.7% in late 2022; about 6.3% in early 2023; about 10.3% in early 2023; about 7.6% in early 2023; about 10.6% in spring 2023; highest point about 13.9% in mid-2023 (highlighted with a small peak mark); about 11.6% after that; about 10.2% and 10.4% in late 2023; about 9.1% in late 2023; about 9.7% and 9.3% in early 2024; about 7.8% in early 2024; about 9.7% in spring 2024; about 11.3% in mid-2024; about 12.3% mid-2024; about 13.1% in late 2024; about 11.4% and about 10.4% at the final point. No legend. Highlighted points: the peak near 13.9% and the late 2024 peak near 13.1% carry small marker accents. Also visible: a decorative soft-pink gradient panel on the right, and an alózcrypto logo bottom-left in light grey outline lettering.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF on the left, with a pink-to-lavender gradient panel (about #FFEAF2 to #E9D5EE) on the right third. White background on the left field and a soft pink-to-lavender gradient on the right panel. Headline text is dark navy #12335A, heavy weight Noto Sans Bold sans-serif, about 4.9% of slide height. Page number is Noto Sans Medium, #999999, about 1.5%. Chart line is dark green about #1E7356 with about 0.5% stroke width and round markers. Gridlines are light grey about #BFBFBF, hairline. Vertical rule is a gradient from purple #7B2CBF at the top to pink #FF6B9D at the bottom, about 0.3% wide. Right panel gradient runs from pale pink #FFEAF2 at top to lavender #E9D5EE at bottom-right. No rounded corners, shadows or borders on the text.

**Visuals**

A left-panel gradient background image with pink-lavender tones and a thin magenta-to-purple vertical stripe; a small grey logo. No photographs.

Interest in crypto-based prediction markets is skyrocketing, / especially around elections / Monthly prediction market volume on Polymarket / $600M / $400M / $200M / $0 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / Presidential forecast / Source: Polymarket.com / 75% / 50% / 25% / 0% / May / Jun / Jul / Aug / Sep / Oct / Trump 50% / Harris 49% / Polymarket / Biden 0% / Swing States / Source: Polymarket.com / Arizona / 11 ELECTORAL VOTES / 63% Trump / 37% Harris / Georgia / 16 ELECTORAL VOTES / 62% Trump / 38% Harris / Pennsylvania / 19 ELECTORAL VOTES / 53% Trump / 47% Harris / Michigan / 15 ELECTORAL VOTES / 53% Harris / 47% Trump / Nevada / 6 ELECTORAL VOTES / 53% Harris / 47% Trump / Wisconsin / 10 ELECTORAL VOTES / 53% Harris / 47% Trump / 25% / Trump ▲1% / Popular Vote Winner / polymarket.com/elections / 74% / Harris ▼1% / Source: Dune (@rchen8) as of 9/30/2024. / 44 / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Full-width header band across the top fifth of the slide holding a two-line left-aligned title with a 'prediction markets' highlight, separated by a gradient rule from a two-column body: left column a bar chart under a bold subtitle, right column a framed Polymarket screenshot, divided by a thin vertical navy rule. Small footer row at the bottom: alzcrypto logo and copyright at left, source line and page number at right, with margins of about 2 to 4 percent of slide width.

**Visuals**

Chart (left): vertical bar chart, monthly prediction market volume on Polymarket. Y axis: US dollars in millions from $0 to $600M, gridlines and labels at $0, $200M, $400M, $600M. X axis: years labelled 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, with about 60 thin monthly bars in dark purple #6B2D7A running from about late 2020 to about October 2024. Values are not printed; estimates: bars 2021 and 2022 mostly about $5M to $30M, 2023 mostly under $10M, early 2024 about $10M to $20M, mid 2024 about $40M to $60M, July 2024 about $110M, August about $385M, September about $455M, latest bar about $505M. No legend; no highlighted points. Screenshot (right): a Polymarket panel showing a Presidential forecast line chart with y axis 0% to 75% and x months May to Oct; a red-orange line Trump 50%, magenta line Harris 49%, blue line Biden 0%, end points marked with dots and tags. Below it a Swing States grid of six cards with paired horizontal bars: Arizona 11 electoral votes Trump 63% and Harris 37%; Georgia 16 Trump 62% and Harris 38%; Pennsylvania 19 Trump 53% and Harris 47%; Michigan 15 Harris 53% and Trump 47%; Nevada 6 Harris 53% and Trump 47%; Wisconsin 10 Harris 53% and Trump 47%. Bottom strip with cropped head-and-shoulders portraits of Donald Trump at left and Kamala Harris at right, a central pill labelled Popular Vote Winner, and figures 25% Trump up 1% and 74% Harris down 1%.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a soft pink-to-lavender gradient header band (about #FDEBF3 at left to #F6E4F4 at right). Title navy #12335A, bold Noto Sans, about 4.9 percent of slide height, with the highlight fill magenta #E032E0 behind white text. Subtitle navy #12335A bold Noto Sans about 2.5 percent. Body and axis labels grey #595959 Noto Sans Medium about 1.6 percent. Accent gradient rule under header from pink #FF6FB0 to purple #7B2CBF, about 0.6 percent high. Vertical divider thin navy #12335A line. Bars dark purple #6B2D7A. Screenshot card white with light grey border and subtle shadow, square-cornered. Footer copyright light grey #D9D9D9, source line grey #595959, page number grey #999999.

### Slide 45

**Text**

VC investments in crypto companies vs. DAO treasury value / $15.0 / billion / $10.0 / billion / Other tokens / $582M / BTC and ETH / $1.9B / Stablecoins / $777M / Own tokens / $11.8B / VC investments in crypto / companies (last 12 months)¹ / Liquid tokens in DAO / treasuries² / DAO / treasuries / have billions / of dollars of / liquid tokens / that can fuel / network / improvements / Sources: 1/ PitchBook as of 9/30/2024 2/ DefiLlama as of 9/30/2024. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide. / 45

**Layout**

Two-column composition: a chart area occupies the left and centre (about 0 to 75 percent of width) and a full-height pink gradient panel with a thin vertical magenta-to-pink rule at about 75 percent of width holds the headline on the right. The chart title sits top-left at about 10 percent of slide height, left-aligned at x 7.3 percent; the headline is left-aligned at x 77.9 percent starting near 7 percent of height. The source line sits bottom-right of the chart area at about 96 percent height, the copyright and logo sit bottom-left, and the page number sits bottom-right at x 97.8 percent.

**Visuals**

Combined bar chart and pie chart with a connecting wedge. Left: bar chart titled none beyond the slide title 'VC investments in crypto companies vs. DAO treasury value'; two vertical bars on a shared baseline with no y-axis, no gridlines and no numeric axis ticks. Bar 1 is dark green #004509-like, labelled '$10.0 billion' in white text on a dark green label box, category 'VC investments in crypto companies (last 12 months)¹'. Bar 2 is light cyan #60D1D7-like, labelled '$15.0 billion' in navy text on a cyan label box, category 'Liquid tokens in DAO treasuries²'. Bar 2 is about 1.5 times the height of bar 1. Middle: a light cyan gradient wedge fading from about #60D1D7 at the pie side to near white at the bar side connects the top-right corner area of bar 2 region to the pie. Right: pie chart with no axes, no legend, slices labelled directly. Values in the pie are printed, not estimated. The cyan 'Own tokens' slice is the largest, roughly 85 percent of the pie area. Headline word 'liquid tokens' is highlighted with a magenta #E032E0 rectangle behind white text.

### Slide 46

**Text**

Decentralized physical infrastructure networks (DePIN) are
underpinning nascent services in the
physical world
Wireless network
Vehicle & mobility data
374,743 active hotspots
113,499 cars connected
Mapping
Energy
1,100+ users on testnet
348 million
kilometers mapped
Weather
Food delivery
13,499 weather stations
224 restaurants in Northern Colorado
46
Some of the above are a16z investments. For a full list see a16z.com/investment-list.
This content should not be considered investment advice.
Sources: Helium, Hivemapper, WeatherXM, Dimo, Daylight, and Nosh as of 9/30/2024.
©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Six equal rounded cards sit in a two-column by three-row grid below a full-width header band, with the title left-aligned at the top and a pink-to-purple rule under it. Each card has a purple category label across its top edge, a logo and a stat on the left half, and a photo filling the right half of the card. The footer has the a16z logo and copyright bottom-left, a two-line footnote centred-left at the bottom, a right-aligned sources line, and the page number 46 at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Six photographs and six company logos, plus one a16z logo in the footer. Top-left card: Helium logo (blue circular hotspot mark with the word helium in blue) above the stat 374,743 active hotspots; right half shows a white Helium hotspot puck on a window sill with a city skyline outside, daylight, shallow depth of field, boxed with rounded corners. Top-right card: DIMO logo (black wordmark) above 113,499 cars connected; right half shows a hand holding a grey device labelled MACARON plugged into a car dashboard, interior lighting, grey and white tones, rounded box. Middle-left card: hivemapper logo (blue wordmark with bird mark) above 348 million kilometers mapped; right half shows a young man driving with his hand raised to a rear-view mirror, phone in a mount, green foliage through the windscreen, natural daylight, rounded box. Middle-right card: Daylight logo (orange starburst mark with black wordmark) above 1,100+ users on testnet; right half shows hands holding a blue PowerX battery pack on a white hexagonal-tile surface with a black product box, bright overhead light, rounded box. Bottom-left card: WeatherXM logo (black wordmark and blue XM mark) above 13,499 weather stations; right half shows a white WeatherXM weather station on a mast against a blue-to-warm sky over a hillside town at sunset, rounded box. Bottom-right card: NOSH logo (black wordmark with red delivery strip) above 224 restaurants in Northern Colorado; right half shows the front of Mary's Mountain Cookies storefront with a large sign reading MARY'S MOUNTAIN COOKIES, a window reading OPEN, green-tiled facade, daylight, rounded box cropped at the card edge. Highlights: yellow rectangles behind the bold numbers, and a magenta rectangle behind the words physical world in the title.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a very pale pink-to-white gradient wash across the header band at the top.

The a16z crypto logo sits bottom-left above the copyright line, and the slide number sits bottom-right; the text is left-aligned with no columns.

**Visuals**

The a16z crypto white wordmark logo sits at bottom-left (x 2.2%, y 93.6%, w 7.6%, h 2.6%). No charts, tables or photographs.

50

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a narrow left panel (about 0 to 30% of width) carries the large bold title stacked in nine lines, left-aligned at about 3.3% from the left edge. The right area (about 33% to 97%) holds a header block at top (section title and italic subtitle), two column headings ('Projects in 2024' over a bubble cluster on the left, 'Top 5 by change in total share' over a table on the right), and footnotes at the bottom left and bottom right. The a16z wordmark and copyright sit bottom-left on the gradient panel; the page number 50 sits bottom-right at about 97.8%.

**Visuals**

Chart: a bubble-cluster diagram titled 'Projects in 2024', drawn as a large circle of circles. Five large bubbles are sized by share and carry labels: Ethereum 20.8% (largest, lilac outline, centre-left), Solana 11.2% (green outline, centre-right), Base 10.7% (royal blue outline, lower centre), Polygon PoS 7.9% (violet outline, upper centre), Optimism 6.7% (red outline, top), Arbitrum 6.2% (light blue outline, lower left), Other blockchains 4.5% (grey outline, upper right), Avalanche 4.2% (red outline, upper left), Bitcoin 4.2% (orange outline, lower right), Polygon zkEVM 1.7% (purple outline, right), zkSync 2.1% (purple outline, lower left), BNB Chain 1.9% (gold outline, top). Around them are about 25 small logo-only bubbles in outlines of red, orange, green, blue, purple, yellow and grey, many marked with a small '+' bubble for further projects; the logos are small and partly unreadable. A thin grey leader line runs from a small purple bubble on the left edge of the cluster up to the top-left of the Optimism bubble. Logos recognisable: Optimism (red circle with white 'OP'), Polygon (violet hexagonal mark), Solana (three-stripe mark), Base (blue sphere), Bitcoin (orange B coin), Ethereum (dark diamond), Arbitrum (dark blue mark), zkSync (black circle mark), Avalanche (red triangle mark), Zora (dark sphere), Polygon zkEVM (violet mark), Polygon-style 'C' in yellow circle, and the BNB Chain gold cube. Table: titled 'Top 5 by change in total share', columns 2023 | arrow of teal dots ending in a teal arrowhead | 2024 | change column with green gradient fill; rows: Solana 5.1% → 11.2% +6.1; Base 7.8% → 10.7% +2.9; Bitcoin 2.6% → 4.2% +1.6; Ethereum 19.7% → 20.8% +1.1; Zora 0.4% → 1.3% +0.9. Values for bubble percentages are printed; no estimates needed.

**Design**

Background: Left 30% soft mint-to-pale-yellow gradient (#C6EDE0 to #FBF8D0); right area solid white #FFFFFF. Left panel: soft vertical gradient from pale mint #C6EDE0 at top to pale yellow #FBF8D0 at bottom, separated from the white right area by a thin vertical line that shifts from teal #1FA08C at top to golden #D9B21E at bottom. Right area white #FFFFFF. Title and bubble labels navy #12335A; body and footnote text grey #595959; table body text dark grey #1F1F1F; headers navy. Change column green gradient from #6AAE3F at top to #CDEEB0 at bottom with white figures. Teal accent #1A9C8A for dotted arrow. Typeface: Noto Sans (Bold for headings and labels, Regular for table and body, Italic for subtitle, Medium for percentages). Title is about 4.9% of slide height, body about 1.4%, headings about 2.8%. **Layout**

Full-width title block across the top in a pale gradient band, with a thin gradient rule beneath it. Below, a left column holds the subtitle and a treemap chart filling the left and centre of the slide, and a right column holds a two-column subcategory table with a source note beneath it. The title starts at about 3.5% from the left edge and the body content sits on the same left margin. The page number sits bottom right at about 97.8% x and 96.2% y, and the copyright line and a16z crypto logo sit bottom left. The slide has no footer bar.

**Visuals**

Treemap chart titled 'Projects in 2024' with 'vs 2023' in a navy box. Each tile is a category, its size proportional to share of builders, and each tile shows its name, percentage share in 2024, and change versus 2023 in a navy box. Tiles, with name, share and change: Decentralized finance (DeFi) 24.8% +3.5 in green #89BD65 (large tile at left, about 3.5% to 18.4% x, 40.6% to 90.9% y); Blockchain infrastructure 19.1% +2.7 in cyan #83EBEC (about 18.9% to 37.6% x, 40.6% to 71.3% y); Picks & shovels 11.3% -2.2 in lilac #F4C1FF (about 18.9% to 37.6% x, 72.4% to 90.9% y); Social 10.3% +0.2 in peach #FFD2A2 (about 38.2% to 53.3% x, 40.6% to 60.3% y); Gaming 6.3% +0.4 in pink #FDA5CB (about 53.7% to 65.5% x, 40.6% to 55.8% y); Media 5.1% -0.5 in slate #9AB3BF (about 53.7% to 65.5% x, 56.8% to 67.3% y); Wallets & identity 8.2% -2.7 in light pink #FFCADC (about 38.2% to 46.9% x, 61.5% to 90.9% y); Physical infra (DePIN) 6.5% +2.5 in mauve #B5A0BF (about 47.4% to 53.3% x, 61.5% to 90.9% y); NFT infra 4.4% -4.3 in sage #7CB9A8 (about 53.7% to 59.0% x, 68.6% to 90.9% y); Other 3.6% +0.3 in light grey #D9D9D9 (about 59.4% to 65.4% x, 68.6% to 84.3% y); Science (DeSci) 1.2% +0.2 in teal #22C4C9 (about 59.4% to 65.3% x, 85.3% to 90.8% y). No axes, no legend, no highlighted points beyond the tiles. Right side: a table titled 'Top subcategories in 2024' with two columns, category and subcategory, and five rows: DeFi | Real world assets (green highlight); DeFi | Payments (green highlight); Blockchain infrastructure | L2 blockchains (cyan highlight); DeFi | Exchange (green highlight); Social | Social networks (peach highlight). Corner logo: a16z crypto wordmark in light grey outline at bottom left. No photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background white with a pale mint to light blue gradient band across the top about 21% of the height, fading from mint #C8EEE0 at left through near-white #EAF8EE to light blue #C5DCE6 at right. A thin rule under the band runs yellow #F5D83A at left to dark green #3E7F5A at right. Titles and headings are navy #12335A in Noto Sans Bold, title about 4.9% of slide height, section headings about 2.8%, subtitle italic about 2.0%, body text about 2.0% in near-black #1F1F1F. Accent highlight behind 'hottest categories' is lime #E2FF6B. Value labels are white text on navy #12335A boxes. Treemap tiles have square corners, no borders, no shadows, with thin white gutters about 0.5% wide between them. Table rules are thin grey #A6A6A6 horizontal lines. Source text is grey #595959 about 1.4%. Page number is #999999 and copyright is #D9D9D9.

### Slide 52

**Text**

Builder activity by applied technology / The technologies that builders are using across categories (boxes show % breakdown by intersection of project category and technology) / Technologies / Projects in 2024 / AI 33.9% / NFTs 28.0% / ZK 16.2% / DAOs 14.7% / MPC 6.2% / vs 2023 / AI 26.9% / NFTs 32.7% / ZK 16.2% / DAOs 16.5% / MPC 7.7% / Blockchain infrastructure / Decentralized finance (DeFi) / Social / Wallets & identity / Picks & shovels / NFT infra / Physical infra (DePIN) / Other / Media / Gaming / Project categories / Builders show increasing interest in AI in 2024, commonly used by infra, DeFi, DePIN, and picks & shovels projects / 52 / Source: a16z crypto’s Builder Energy Dashboard is based on data from thousands of crypto projects we’ve tracked over the past two years, including investment team research, our CSX startup accelerator program, and other industry-wide tracking through Sept 2024. Please note this does not include all builders or founders and is meant to be for informational purposes only. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide.

**Layout**

Two zones: a white left area holding a chart-style title block, a mosaic chart with row labels on the left and a source line below, and a narrow right panel (about 30 percent of the width) carrying the large headline, separated by a thin gradient divider at about 75 percent of the width. The title sits top-left at about 3.6 percent from the top, left-aligned with the subtitle and chart at a margin of about 2 percent; the page number sits bottom-right in the right panel and the logo and copyright sit bottom-left.

**Visuals**

One Marimekko-style mosaic chart (chart type: mosaic/marimekko, 100 percent stacked columns with variable widths). Chart title: none beyond the slide title. Horizontal axis: Technologies, with five column headers printed as AI 33.9%, NFTs 28.0%, ZK 16.2%, DAOs 14.7%, MPC 6.2% (column widths follow these shares). Sub-header bars under the columns print AI 26.9%, NFTs 32.7%, ZK 16.2%, DAOs 16.5%, MPC 7.7%, labelled vs 2023. Vertical axis: Project categories, ten rows from top to bottom: Blockchain infrastructure (cyan), Decentralized finance (DeFi) (green), Social (peach), Wallets & identity (pink), Picks & shovels (lilac), NFT infra (teal-green), Physical infra (DePIN) (mauve), Other (light grey), Media (blue-grey), Gaming (pink). Each cell is a segment whose height is its share of the column; cell percentages are not printed, so values are about. Cell fills in the AI column, top to bottom: Blockchain about 36 percent of column height (#83EBEC), DeFi about 15 percent (#89BD65), Social about 10 percent (#FFD2A2), Wallets about 10 percent (#FFCADC), Picks about 12 percent (#F4C1FF), NFT infra about 5 percent (#7CB9A8), Physical infra about 12 percent (#B5A0BF), Other about 8 percent (#D9D9D9), Media about 4 percent (#9AB3BF), Gaming about 4 percent (#FDA5CB). NFTs column, top to bottom: Blockchain about 8 percent, DeFi about 15 percent, Social about 22 percent, Wallets about 9 percent, Picks about 10 percent, NFT infra about 15 percent, Physical infra about 2 percent, Other about 4 percent, Media about 10 percent, Gaming about 7 percent. ZK column, top to bottom: Blockchain about 42 percent, DeFi about 14 percent, Social about 6 percent, Wallets about 14 percent, Picks about 9 percent, NFT infra about 4 percent, Physical infra about 7 percent, Other about 4 percent, Media about 7 percent?, Gaming about 2 percent (approximate ordering from image). DAOs column, top to bottom: Blockchain about 17 percent, DeFi about 23 percent, Social about 19 percent, Wallets about 9 percent, Picks about 9 percent, NFT infra about 5 percent, Physical infra about 5 percent, Other about 5 percent, Media about 8 percent, Gaming about 1 percent. MPC column, top to bottom: Blockchain about 16 percent, DeFi about 26 percent, Social about 5 percent, Wallets about 21 percent, Picks about 5 percent, NFT infra about 4 percent, Physical infra about 4 percent, Other about 4 percent, Gaming about 8 percent (Media absent or minimal). Legend: none; row colours serve as the key through the row labels. Highlighted points: none. Right panel: no chart; a gradient image fills it.

**Design**

Background: Left area solid white #FFFFFF; right panel soft gradient from mint #CDEFE3 at top to pale yellow #FFF9D6 at bottom. Clean corporate infographic. Background white on the left, with a soft gradient mint to pale yellow in the right panel. Title and headline text navy #12335A, bold Noto Sans (sans-serif, bold weight), title about 2.5 percent of slide height, headline about 4.9 percent, chart labels about 2.0 percent in Noto Sans Bold and Regular. Subtitle in Noto Sans Italic navy #12335A. Column header bars light grey #C4C4C4 with navy text; sub-header bars dark navy #12335A with light grey #D9D9D9 text. Row label boxes are white with thin coloured outlines and a solid coloured tab on the right. Mosaic cells are flat fills with thin white gaps, square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Source text grey #595959 Regular about 1.4 percent. Copyright #D9D9D9 Medium about 1.2 percent. Page number #999999 Medium about 1.5 percent. Divider line uses a teal to gold gradient, about 0.4 percent wide.

### Slide 53

**Text**

Crypto is / international, / with builders / spread across / the U.S., Asia, / and Europe / Builders by geography / Where founders say they’re based / Top 5 by change in total share / 2023 / 2024 / 8.3% / NY / 13.4% / +5.1 / 11.2% / US / (other) / 15.2% / +4.0 / 8.0% / SF / 11.0% / +3.0 / 3.6% / LA / 5.4% / +1.8 / 3.9% / Canada / 5.4% / +1.5 / Projects in 2024 / UK / 7.8% / Canada / 5.4% / Europe / (Other) / 11.5% / Germany / 1.8% / SF / 11.0% / US (Other) / 15.2% / NY / 13.4% / LA / 5.4% / Miami / 1.7% / France / 1.0% / Israel / 1.0% / Asia / (Other) / 11.2% / Middle / East / 2.6% / India / 5.4% / Africa / 1.5% / LatAm / 2.9% / Oceania / 1.2% / Source: a16z crypto’s Builder Energy Dashboard is based on data from thousands of crypto projects we’ve / tracked over the past two years, including investment team research, our CSX startup accelerator / program, and other industry-wide tracking through Sept 2024. Please note this does not include all / builders or founders and is meant to be for informational purposes only. / Circles scaled non-linearly for readability. / ©2024 Andreessen Horowitz. | All rights reserved worldwide. / 53

**Layout**

Two-column split: a narrow left panel (about 0 to 30% of width) carries the large headline and the copyright line, separated by a thin vertical gradient bar at x 29.7%. The right content area starts with the 'Builders by geography' heading at top-left (x 31.8%, y 12%) and a boxed top-five table at top-right (x 75.2 to 97.4%, y 11.3 to 52.1%); the world map fills the lower right from y 18% down and is cropped at the right and bottom edges. The source note sits bottom-right above the page number at x 97.8%, y 96.2%, and the 'Circles scaled' note sits bottom-left of the content area; the copyright is bottom-left in the left panel.

**Visuals**

A bubble map of builder share by region on a grey world map, plus a small table. Map: grey land (#C4C4C4) on white, showing North America, South America, Europe, Africa, Asia, Australia and Greenland. Seventeen pale lime circles sit over the regions, each labelled with a name and a percentage of projects in 2024, scaled non-linearly: UK 7.8% (north-west Europe, above Germany), Canada 5.4% (central Canada), SF 11.0% (California coast), LA 5.4% (south-west US, under SF), US (Other) 15.2% (central US), NY 13.4% (north-east US), Miami 1.7% (Florida), Germany 1.8%, France 1.0%, Europe (Other) 11.5% (central and eastern Europe), Israel 1.0%, Middle East 2.6%, India 5.4%, Asia (Other) 11.2% (east Asia), Africa 1.5%, LatAm 2.9% (South America), Oceania 1.2% (Australia). Three thin dark-grey leader lines run from the UK bubble and from the Germany and France bubbles to the Europe area. Table (top-right, 'Top 5 by change in total share', 2023 to 2024 with a dotted teal arrow): columns are 2023 share, region, 2024 share, change in points. Rows: 8.3% | NY | 13.4% | +5.1; 11.2% | US (other) | 15.2% | +4.0; 8.0% | SF | 11.0% | +3.0; 3.6% | LA | 5.4% | +1.8; 3.9% | Canada | 5.4% | +1.5. The change column is a vertical gradient strip from dark green to pale green with white values. No photographs or icons apart from the logo.

**Design**

Background: Left panel mint-to-cream gradient (#BDEBDD top-left to #FDF9D6 bottom) over a solid white #FFFFFF right area and white base. Clean corporate look. The left panel has a soft mint-to-cream gradient; the rest is white. Headline and headings are bold navy; body and table text is near-black; the highlight behind 'international,' is lime yellow; bubbles are pale lime with a slightly darker olive edge; the change column uses green. Typeface is Noto Sans (Bold for headings and figures, Regular for labels and table text, Italic for the subtitle). Headline is about 4.9% of slide height; table and map labels are about 1.5 to 2.5%; source text about 1.4%. No rounded corners, shadows or outlines on text boxes; the table box has a thin grey border.

**Layout**

Two-zone composition on a 16:9 slide: a left text column holds the large three-line title, the lilac button and the logo and copyright footer, while a laptop render bleeds off the right edge and sits on an orange block in the lower right. The title is left-aligned at about 4.3% from the left edge, starts near 14% from the top and is stacked on three lines. The source note sits in the bottom right above the page number at about 59.7% from the left, and the copyright line is at the bottom left at about 2.1% from the left.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed 3D render of an open silver laptop, screen angled toward the viewer, resting on the right side of a matte orange box or plinth that runs off the bottom edge; the laptop's right side and screen are cropped by the right slide edge. Laptop screen (a boxed screenshot, 60.8% to 100% wide, top at 0.7%) shows the a16z crypto Builder Energy dashboard: a green-to-pink gradient header with the BUILDER ENERGY wordmark in black letters with a yellow highlight, a light grey panel with a bubble chart titled Builder interest by blockchain, and a bar list titled Builder interest (Top 10). Bubbles are outlined in red, blue, purple, green and orange, with the largest labelled ETHEREUM (partly cut off), OPTIMISM 6.7%, POLYGON POS 7.9%, AVALANCHE 4.2%, BNB CHAIN 1.9%, OTHER BLOCKCHAIN 4.5% and POLYGON ZKEVM 1.7%; SOLANA is cut off at the bottom edge. The bar list shows outlined bars labelled Polygon PoS 7.9% and Optimism. Lighting is soft and diffuse from the upper left, casting faint shadows under the laptop. Dominant colours are light grey, orange (#C8660F to #B5530A), silver and the screenshot's green and pink gradient. No logos other than the a16z crypto wordmark in the screenshot and footer. Lilac button (#D0A1FF) overlay sits on the left, with a white highlight strip behind the link text.

**Design**

Background: Light grey gradient (#C8C8C8 at top-left to #F2F2F2 toward centre-right) with the 3D laptop and orange plinth rendered across the lower right.

55

**Layout**

Left-aligned text block in the left half of a full-bleed image. The small section line sits at about 25% height, the two-line title is set large below it, and the logo and copyright run along the bottom-left margin with the slide number at the bottom-right. No grid or column structure is visible, and the title sits in the middle third of the slide.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed illustration covering the entire slide: a mosaic of square tiles on a gradient field. Tile rows and columns are about 5% of slide width and height, with a slight brightness shift per tile. Colour runs from green at top-left (#00A862 to #2ED27A) through pale yellow-green and peach at top-centre to pale pink at top-right (#FFC0CB). The left-middle is lime green (#7ED957), the centre is salmon and pink (#F28C9A to #FF4FA0), and the right side deepens from magenta (#D63FA5) to purple (#8A1A8C). The bottom-left is bright yellow (#F2E83A) shading into orange (#F9A36B) and hot pink (#FF3C9E) at bottom-centre, with dark indigo (#2E0070) at bottom-right. No people, objects or text are in the image. The image is not cropped and has no overlay. Lighting is flat and even. A grey logo wordmark 'al6zcrypto' sits at bottom-left over the yellow area.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed multicolour gradient mosaic with no border. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in NotoSans-ExtraBold (sans-serif, extra-bold weight) with tight leading, about 14.3% of slide height per line. The section line is black (#000000) in NotoSans-Medium (sans-serif, medium weight) at about 3.0% of slide height. Copyright text is light grey (#B7B7B7) in NotoSans-Medium at about 1.2%. Page number is off-white (#EBEBEB) in NotoSans-Medium at about 1.5%. No shapes, lines, corner radii, shadows or extra gradients are present beyond the background mosaic.
